




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

mātikā

1. tikamātikā

1. (ka) kusalā  dhammā|

(kha) akusalā dhammā|

(ga) abyākatā dhammā|

2. (ka) sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttā dhammā|

(kha) dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā dhammā|

(ga) adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā dhammā|

3. (ka) vipākā dhammā|

(kha) vipākadhammadhammā|

(ga) naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā|

4. (ka) upādiṇṇupādāniyā upādin‍nupādāniyā (syā॰) dhammā|

(kha) anupādiṇṇupādāniyā dhammā|

(ga) anupādiṇṇaanupādāniyā anupādin‍nānupādāniyā (syā॰) dhammā|

5. (ka) saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā dhammā|

6. (ka) savitak‍kasavicārā  dhammā|

(ga) upaikkhāsahagatā dhammā|

8. (ka) dassanaina pahātabbā dhammā|

(ga) naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbā dhammā|

9. (ka) dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā dhammā|

(ga) maggādhipatinau dhammā|

(kha) haitusampayuttā caiva dhammā na ca haitū|

6. (ka) na haitū khau pana dhammā sahaitukāpi|

(kha) ahaitukāpi|

1. cittuppādakaṇḍaṃ

kāmāvacarakusalaṃ

padabhājanī

1. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, pīti hauti, sukhaṃ hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, saddhindriyaṃ hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ viriyindriyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) hauti, satindriyaṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, saumanassindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, sammādiṭṭhi hauti, sammāsaṅkappau hauti, sammāvāyāmau hauti, sammāsati hauti, sammāsamādhi hauti, saddhābalaṃ hauti, vīriyabalaṃ viriyabalaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) hauti, satibalaṃ hauti, samādhibalaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñābalaṃ hauti, hiribalaṃ hauti, auttappabalaṃ hauti, alaubhau hauti, adausau hauti, amauhau hauti, anabhijjhā hauti, abyāpādau hauti, sammādiṭṭhi hauti, hirī hauti, auttappaṃ hauti, kāyapassaddhi kāyappassaddhi (syā॰) hauti, cittapassaddhi cittappassaddhi (syā॰) hauti, kāyalahutā hauti, cittalahutā hauti, kāyamudutā hauti, cittamudutā hauti, kāyakammañ‍ñatā hauti, cittakammañ‍ñatā hauti, kāyapāguñ‍ñatā hauti, cittapāguñ‍ñatā hauti, kāyujukatā kāyuj‍jukatā (sī॰ ka॰) hauti, cittujukatā  cittuj‍jukatā (sī॰ ka॰) hauti, sati hauti, sampajañ‍ñaṃ hauti, samathau hauti, vipassanā hauti, paggāhau hauti, avikkhaipau hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā|

2. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti|

3. katamā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ  sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti|

4. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā sañ‍jānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti|

5. katamā tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā caitanā sañ‍caitanā caitayitattaṃ sañ‍caitayitattaṃ (syā॰) – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti|

6. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti|

7. katamau tasmiṃ samayai vitak‍kau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai tak‍kau  vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā sammāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vitak‍kau hauti|

8. katamau tasmiṃ samayai vicārau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai cārau vicārau anuvicārau upavicārau cittassa anusandhānatā anupaikkhanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vicārau hauti|

9. katamā tasmiṃ samayai pīti hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pīti pāmauj‍jaṃ āmaudanā pamaudanā hāsau pahāsau vitti audagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pīti hauti|

10. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ hauti|

11. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti|

12. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saddhindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai saddhā saddahanā aukappanā abhippasādau  saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saddhindriyaṃ hauti|

13. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyindriyaṃ hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau viriyārambhau (sī॰ syā॰) nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau  ussauḷhī ussauḷhi (sī॰) thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyindriyaṃ hauti|

14. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai satindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai satindriyaṃ hauti|

15. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhindriyaṃ hauti|

16. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā  pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti|

17. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti|

18. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saumanassindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saumanassindriyaṃ hauti|

19. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti? yau taisaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā irīyanā (sī॰)  vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti|

20. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sammādiṭṭhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammādiṭṭhi hauti|

21. katamau tasmiṃ samayai sammāsaṅkappau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai tak‍kau vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā sammāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāsaṅkappau hauti|

22. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai sammāvāyāmau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāvāyāmau hauti|

23. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sammāsati hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāsati hauti|

24. katamau tasmiṃ samayai sammāsamādhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāsamādhi hauti|

25. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saddhābalaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai saddhā saddahanā aukappanā abhippasādau saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saddhābalaṃ hauti|

26. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyabalaṃ hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau  vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau – idaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai vīriyabalaṃ hauti|

27. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai satibalaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai satibalaṃ hauti|

28. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhibalaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhibalaṃ hauti|

29. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñābalaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau  pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñābalaṃ hauti|

30. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai hiribalaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai hirīyati hiriyitabbaina hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai hiribalaṃ hauti|

31. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai auttappabalaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai auttappati  auttappitabbaina auttappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai auttappabalaṃ hauti|

32. katamau tasmiṃ samayai alaubhau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai alaubhau alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgau asāraj‍janā asāraj‍jitattaṃ anabhijjhā alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai alaubhau hauti|

33. katamau tasmiṃ samayai adausau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai adausau adussanā adussitattaṃ adūsanā adūsitattaṃ (syā॰) abyāpādau abyāpaj‍jau adausau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai adausau hauti?

34. katamau tasmiṃ samayai amauhau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau  sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi amauhau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai amauhau hauti|

35. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai anabhijjhā hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai alaubhau alubbhanā  alubbhitattaṃ asārāgau asāraj‍janā asāraj‍jitattaṃ anabhijjhā alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai anabhijjhā hauti|

36. katamau tasmiṃ samayai abyāpādau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai adausau  adussanā adussitattaṃ abyāpādau abyāpaj‍jau adausau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai abyāpādau hauti|

37. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sammādiṭṭhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammādiṭṭhi hauti|

38. katamā tasmiṃ samayai hirī hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai hirīyati hiriyitabbaina hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai hirī hauti|

39. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai auttappaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai auttappati auttappitabbaina auttappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai auttappaṃ hauti|

40. katamā tasmiṃ samayai kāyapassaddhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhassa sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa passaddhi paṭipassaddhi paṭippassaddhi (sī॰ syā॰) passambhanā paṭipassambhanā paṭippassambhanā (sī॰ syā॰) paṭipassambhitattaṃ paṭippassambhitattaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyapassaddhi hauti|

41. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittapassaddhi hauti? yā  tasmiṃ samayai  viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa passaddhi paṭipassaddhi passambhanā paṭipassambhanā paṭipassambhitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittapassaddhi hauti|

42. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai kāyalahutā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhassa sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa lahutā lahupariṇāmatā adandhanatā avitthanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyalahutā hauti|

43. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittalahutā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa lahutā lahupariṇāmatā adandhanatā avitthanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittalahutā hauti|

44. katamā tasmiṃ samayai kāyamudutā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhassa sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa mudutā maddavatā akakkhaḷatā akathinatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyamudutā hauti|

45. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittamudutā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa mudutā maddavatā akakkhaḷatā akathinatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittamudutā hauti|

46. katamā tasmiṃ samayai kāyakammañ‍ñatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhassa sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa kammañ‍ñatā kammañ‍ñattaṃ kammañ‍ñabhāvau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyakammañ‍ñatā hauti|

47. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai cittakammañ‍ñatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa kammañ‍ñatā kammañ‍ñattaṃ kammañ‍ñabhāvau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittakammañ‍ñatā hauti|

48. katamā tasmiṃ samayai kāyapāguñ‍ñatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhassa sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa paguṇatā paguṇattaṃ paguṇabhāvau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyapāguñ‍ñatā hauti|

49. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittapāguñ‍ñatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa paguṇatā paguṇattaṃ paguṇabhāvau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittapāguñ‍ñatā hauti|

50. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai kāyujukatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhassa sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa  ujutā ujukatā ajimhatā avaṅkatā akuṭilatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyujukatā hauti|

51. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai cittujukatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa ujutā ujukatā ajimhatā avaṅkatā akuṭilatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittujukatā hauti|

52. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sati hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sati hauti|

53. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sampajañ‍ñaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā  paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sampajañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

54. katamau tasmiṃ samayai samathau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samathau hauti|

55. katamā tasmiṃ samayai vipassanā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vipassanā hauti|

56. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai paggāhau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā  anikkhittadhuratā  dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai paggāhau hauti|

57. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai avikkhaipau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai avikkhaipau hauti|

yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā|

padabhājanīyaṃ|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau|

kauṭṭhāsavārau

58. tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, aṭṭhindriyāni haunti, pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti, pañ‍caṅgikau maggau hauti, satta balāni haunti, tayau haitū haunti, aikau phassau hauti, aikā vaidanā hauti, aikā sañ‍ñā hauti, aikā caitanā hauti, aikaṃ cittaṃ hauti, aikau vaidanākkhandhau hauti, aikau sañ‍ñākkhandhau hauti, aikau saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti, aikau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau hauti, aikaṃ manāyatanaṃ hauti, aikaṃ manindriyaṃ hauti, aikā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti, aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā|

59. katamai  tasmiṃ samayai cattārau khandhā haunti? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

60. katamau tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhau hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ  vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhau hauti|

61. katamau tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñākkhandhau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā sañ‍jānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñākkhandhau hauti|

62. katamau tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau pīti cittassaikaggatā saddhindriyaṃ  vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ hiribalaṃ auttappabalaṃ alaubhau adausau amauhau anabhijjhā abyāpādau sammādiṭṭhi hirī auttappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammañ‍ñatā cittakammañ‍ñatā kāyapāguñ‍ñatā cittapāguñ‍ñatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati sampajañ‍ñaṃ samathau vipassanā paggāhau avikkhaipau; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā thapaitvā (ka॰) vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti|

63. katamau tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau hauti|

imai tasmiṃ samayai cattārau khandhā haunti|

64. katamāni tasmiṃ samayai dvāyatanāni haunti? manāyatanaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ|

65. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai manāyatanaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manāyatanaṃ hauti|

66. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai dhammāyatanaṃ hauti? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai dhammāyatanaṃ hauti|

imāni tasmiṃ samayai dvāyatanāni haunti|

67. katamā tasmiṃ samayai dvai dhātuyau haunti? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu|

68. katamā tasmiṃ samayai manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti|

69. katamā tasmiṃ samayai dhammadhātu hauti? vaidanākkhandhau,  sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai dhammadhātu hauti|

imā tasmiṃ samayai dvai dhātuyau haunti|

70. katamai tasmiṃ samayai tayau āhārā haunti? phassāhārau, manausañ‍caitanāhārau, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau|

71. katamau tasmiṃ samayai phassāhārau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassāhārau hauti|

72. katamau tasmiṃ samayai manausañ‍caitanāhārau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai caitanā sañ‍caitanā caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manausañ‍caitanāhārau hauti|

73. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇāhārau hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇāhārau hauti|

imai tasmiṃ samayai tayau āhārā haunti|

74. katamāni  tasmiṃ samayai aṭṭhindriyāni haunti? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ|

75. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saddhindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai saddhā saddahanā aukappanā abhippasādau saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saddhindriyaṃ hauti|

76. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyindriyaṃ hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyindriyaṃ hauti|

77. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai satindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā  apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai satindriyaṃ hauti|

78. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ  sammāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhindriyaṃ hauti|

79. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā  pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti|

80. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti|

81. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai saumanassindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saumanassindriyaṃ hauti|

82. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti? yau taisaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti|

imāni tasmiṃ samayai aṭṭhindriyāni haunti|

83. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti? vitak‍kau, vicārau, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā|

84. katamau tasmiṃ samayai vitak‍kau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai tak‍kau vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā sammāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vitak‍kau hauti|

85. katamau tasmiṃ samayai vicārau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai cārau vicārau anuvicārau upavicārau cittassa anusandhānatā anupaikkhanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vicārau hauti|

86. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai pīti hauti? yā  tasmiṃ samayai pīti pāmauj‍jaṃ āmaudanā pamaudanā hāsau pahāsau vitti audagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pīti hauti|

87. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ hauti|

88. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti|

idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti|

89. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍caṅgikau maggau hauti? sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

90. katamā tasmiṃ  samayai sammādiṭṭhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammādiṭṭhi hauti|

91. katamau tasmiṃ samayai sammāsaṅkappau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai tak‍kau vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā sammāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāsaṅkappau hauti|

92. katamau tasmiṃ samayai sammāvāyāmau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau  nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāvāyāmau hauti|

93. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sammāsati hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā  apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāsati hauti|

94. katamau tasmiṃ samayai sammāsamādhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi  – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāsamādhi hauti|

ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍caṅgikau maggau hauti|

95. katamāni  tasmiṃ samayai satta balāni haunti? saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ|

96. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saddhābalaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai saddhā saddahanā aukappanā abhippasādau saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saddhābalaṃ hauti|

97. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyabalaṃ hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyabalaṃ hauti|

98. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai satibalaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai satibalaṃ hauti|

99. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai samādhibalaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhibalaṃ hauti|

100. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñābalaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā  vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau  pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñābalaṃ hauti|

101. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai hiribalaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai hirīyati hiriyitabbaina hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai hiribalaṃ hauti|

102. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai auttappabalaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai auttappati auttappitabbaina auttappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai auttappabalaṃ hauti|

imāni tasmiṃ samayai satta balāni haunti|

103. katamai tasmiṃ samayai tayau haitū haunti? alaubhau, adausau, amauhau|

104. katamau tasmiṃ samayai alaubhau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai alaubhau alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgau asāraj‍janā asāraj‍jitattaṃ anabhijjhā alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai alaubhau hauti|

105. katamau tasmiṃ samayai adausau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai adausau adussanā adussitattaṃ  abyāpādau abyāpaj‍jau adausau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai adausau hauti|

106. katamau tasmiṃ samayai amauhau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰…  amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai amauhau hauti|

imai tasmiṃ samayai tayau haitū haunti|

107. katamau tasmiṃ samayai aikau phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikau phassau hauti|

108. katamā tasmiṃ samayai aikā vaidanā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikā vaidanā hauti|

109. katamā tasmiṃ samayai aikā sañ‍ñā hauti? yā  tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā sañ‍jānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikā sañ‍ñā hauti|

110. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai aikā caitanā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai caitanā sañ‍caitanā caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikā caitanā hauti|

111. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai aikaṃ cittaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikaṃ cittaṃ hauti|

112. katamau tasmiṃ samayai aikau vaidanākkhandhau hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikau vaidanākkhandhau hauti|

113. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai aikau sañ‍ñākkhandhau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā sañ‍jānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikau sañ‍ñākkhandhau hauti|

114. katamau tasmiṃ samayai aikau saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau pīti cittassaikaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ hiribalaṃ auttappabalaṃ alaubhau adausau amauhau anabhijjhā abyāpādau sammādiṭṭhi hirī auttappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammañ‍ñatā cittakammañ‍ñatā kāyapāguñ‍ñatā cittapāguñ‍ñatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati sampajañ‍ñaṃ samathau vipassanā  paggāhau avikkhaipau; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikau saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti|

115. katamau tasmiṃ samayai aikau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau hauti|

116. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai aikaṃ manāyatanaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikaṃ manāyatanaṃ hauti|

117. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikaṃ manindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikaṃ manindriyaṃ hauti|

118. katamā tasmiṃ samayai aikā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau  mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti|

119. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti|

120. katamā tasmiṃ samayai aikā dhammadhātu hauti? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai aikā dhammadhātu hauti|

yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā|

kauṭṭhāsavārau|

suñ‍ñatavārau

121. tasmiṃ khau pana samayai dhammā haunti, khandhā haunti, āyatanāni haunti, dhātuyau haunti, āhārā haunti, indriyāni  haunti, jhānaṃ hauti, maggau hauti, balāni haunti, haitū haunti, phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vaidanākkhandhau hauti, sañ‍ñākkhandhau hauti, saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau hauti, manāyatanaṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti, dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā|

122. katamai tasmiṃ samayai dhammā haunti? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – imai tasmiṃ samayai dhammā haunti|

123. katamai  tasmiṃ samayai khandhā haunti? vaidanākkhandhau,  sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – imai tasmiṃ samayai khandhā haunti|

124. katamāni tasmiṃ samayai āyatanāni haunti? manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – imāni tasmiṃ samayai āyatanāni haunti|

125. katamā tasmiṃ samayai dhātuyau haunti? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu – imā tasmiṃ samayai dhātuyau haunti|

126. katamai tasmiṃ samayai āhārā haunti? phassāhārau, manausañ‍caitanāhārau, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau – imai tasmiṃ samayai āhārā haunti|

127. katamāni tasmiṃ samayai indriyāni haunti? saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ – imāni tasmiṃ samayai indriyāni haunti|

128. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jhānaṃ hauti? vitak‍kau, vicārau, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jhānaṃ hauti|

129. katamau tasmiṃ samayai maggau hauti? sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai maggau hauti|

130. katamāni  tasmiṃ samayai balāni haunti? saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, auttappabalaṃ – imāni tasmiṃ samayai balāni haunti|

131. katamai  tasmiṃ samayai haitū haunti? alaubhau, adausau, amauhau – imai tasmiṃ samayai haitū haunti|

132. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti|

paṭhamaṃ cittaṃ|

dutiyaṃ cittaṃ|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, sattindriyāni haunti, pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti, caturaṅgikau maggau hauti, cha balāni haunti, dvai haitū haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā…pai॰…|

148. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau pīti cittassaikaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammāsaṅkappau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ hiribalaṃ auttappabalaṃ alaubhau adausau anabhijjhā abyāpādau hirī auttappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammañ‍ñatā cittakammañ‍ñatā kāyapāguñ‍ñatā cittapāguñ‍ñatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati samathau paggāhau avikkhaipau; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

tatiyaṃ cittaṃ|

149. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ  vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

catutthaṃ cittaṃ|

150. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, saddhindriyaṃ hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ hauti, satindriyaṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, sammādiṭṭhi hauti, sammāsaṅkappau hauti, sammāvāyāmau hauti, sammāsati hauti, sammāsamādhi hauti, saddhābalaṃ hauti, vīriyabalaṃ hauti, satibalaṃ hauti, samādhibalaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñābalaṃ hauti, hiribalaṃ hauti, auttappabalaṃ hauti, alaubhau hauti, adausau hauti, amauhau hauti, anabhijjhā hauti, abyāpādau hauti, sammādiṭṭhi hauti, hirī hauti, auttappaṃ hauti, kāyapassaddhi hauti, cittapassaddhi hauti, kāyalahutā hauti, cittalahutā hauti, kāyamudutā hauti, cittamudutā hauti, kāyakammañ‍ñatā hauti, cittakammañ‍ñatā hauti kāyapāguñ‍ñatā  hauti, cittapāguñ‍ñatā hauti, kāyujukatā hauti, cittujukatā hauti, sati hauti, sampajañ‍ñaṃ hauti, samathau hauti, vipassanā hauti, paggāhau hauti, avikkhaipau hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā|

151. katamau tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti|

152. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ  caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti…pai॰…|

153. katamā tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhā hauti…pai॰…|

154. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā…pai॰…|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, aṭṭhindriyāni haunti, caturaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti, pañ‍caṅgikau maggau hauti, satta balāni haunti, tayau haitū haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā…pai॰…|

155. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau cittassaikaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ hiribalaṃ auttappabalaṃ alaubhau adausau amauhau anabhijjhā abyāpādau sammādiṭṭhi hirī auttappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammañ‍ñatā cittakammañ‍ñatā kāyapāguñ‍ñatā cittapāguñ‍ñatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati sampajañ‍ñaṃ samathau vipassanā paggāhau avikkhaipau|

yai  vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ  ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

pañ‍camaṃ cittaṃ|

156. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

chaṭṭhaṃ cittaṃ|

157. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, saddhindriyaṃ hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ hauti, satindriyaṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, sammāsaṅkappau hauti, sammāvāyāmau hauti, sammāsati hauti, sammāsamādhi hauti, saddhābalaṃ hauti, vīriyabalaṃ hauti, satibalaṃ hauti, samādhibalaṃ hauti, hiribalaṃ hauti, auttappabalaṃ hauti, alaubhau hauti, adausau hauti, anabhijjhā hauti, abyāpādau hauti, hirī hauti, auttappaṃ hauti, kāyapassaddhi hauti, cittapassaddhi hauti, kāyalahutā hauti, cittalahutā hauti, kāyamudutā hauti, cittamudutā hauti, kāyakammañ‍ñatā hauti, cittakammañ‍ñatā hauti, kāyapāguñ‍ñatā hauti, cittapāguñ‍ñatā hauti, kāyujukatā hauti, cittujukatā hauti, sati hauti, samathau hauti, paggāhau hauti, avikkhaipau  hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā…pai॰…|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, sattindriyāni haunti, caturaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti, caturaṅgikau maggau hauti, cha balāni haunti, dvai haitū haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā…pai॰…|

158. katamau tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau cittassaikaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammāsaṅkappau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ hiribalaṃ auttappabalaṃ alaubhau adausau anabhijjhā abyāpādau hirī auttappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammañ‍ñatā cittakammañ‍ñatā kāyapāguñ‍ñatā cittapāguñ‍ñatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati samathau paggāhau avikkhaipau|

yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā  sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

174. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ pañ‍camajjhānaṃ (sī॰) upasampaj‍ja viharati  pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, saddhindriyaṃ hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ hauti, satindriyaṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, sammādiṭṭhi hauti, sammāvāyāmau hauti…pai॰… paggāhau hauti, avikkhaipau hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā…pai॰…|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, aṭṭhindriyāni haunti, duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti, caturaṅgikau maggau hauti, satta balāni haunti, tayau haitū haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā  pana tasmiṃ samayai  añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā…pai॰…|

175. katamau tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā cittassaikaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāvāyāmau…pai॰… paggāhau avikkhaipau; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

pañ‍cakanayau|

catassau paṭipadā

273. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ  amanasikārā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… chandādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… cittādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… chandādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… cittādhipataiyyaṃ  hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

274. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… chandādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… cittādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… chandādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰…  vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… cittādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

275. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… chandādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… cittādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… chandādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… cittādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

276. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… chandādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… cittādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… chandādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰…  vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ  hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… cittādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ…pai॰… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ…pai॰… majjhimaṃ…pai॰… paṇītaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

arūpāvacarakusalaṃ|

laukuttarakusalaṃ

suddhikapaṭipadā

277. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, pīti hauti, sukhaṃ hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, saddhindriyaṃ hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ hauti, satindriyaṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, saumanassindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ hauti, sammādiṭṭhi hauti, sammāsaṅkappau hauti, sammāvācā hauti, sammākammantau hauti, sammāājīvau hauti, sammāvāyāmau hauti, sammāsati hauti, sammāsamādhi hauti, saddhābalaṃ hauti, vīriyabalaṃ hauti, satibalaṃ hauti, samādhibalaṃ hauti, pañ‍ñābalaṃ hauti, hiribalaṃ hauti, auttappabalaṃ hauti, alaubhau hauti, adausau hauti, amauhau  hauti, anabhijjhā hauti, abyāpādau hauti, sammādiṭṭhi hauti, hirī hauti, auttappaṃ hauti, kāyapassaddhi hauti, cittapassaddhi hauti, kāyalahutā hauti, cittalahutā hauti, kāyamudutā hauti, cittamudutā hauti, kāyakammañ‍ñatā hauti, cittakammañ‍ñatā hauti, kāyapāguñ‍ñatā hauti, cittapāguñ‍ñatā hauti, kāyujukatā hauti, cittujukatā hauti, sati hauti, sampajañ‍ñaṃ hauti, samathau hauti, vipassanā hauti, paggāhau  hauti, avikkhaipau hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā|

278. katamau tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti|

279. katamā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ  caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti|

280. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā sañ‍jānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti|

281. katamā tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā caitanā sañ‍caitanā caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti|

282. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti|

283. katamau tasmiṃ samayai vitak‍kau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai tak‍kau vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā sammāsaṅkappau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vitak‍kau hauti|

284. katamau tasmiṃ samayai vicārau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai cārau vicārau anuvicārau upavicārau cittassa anusandhānatā anupaikkhanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vicārau hauti|

285. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai pīti hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pīti pāmauj‍jaṃ āmaudanā pamaudanā hāsau pahāsau vitti audagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa pītisambaujjhaṅgau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pīti hauti|

286. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ hauti|

287. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai  cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti|

288. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai saddhindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai saddhā saddahanā aukappanā abhippasādau saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saddhindriyaṃ hauti|

289. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyindriyaṃ hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyindriyaṃ hauti|

290. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai satindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai satindriyaṃ hauti|

291. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā  samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau  maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhindriyaṃ hauti|

292. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau  pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti|

293. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti|

294. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saumanassindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saumanassindriyaṃ hauti|

295. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti? yau taisaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ  jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti|

296. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ hauti? yā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ anañ‍ñātānaṃ adiṭṭhānaṃ appattānaṃ aviditānaṃ asacchikatānaṃ sacchikiriyāya pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā  pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ hauti|

297. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sammādiṭṭhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau  pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammādiṭṭhi hauti|

298. katamau tasmiṃ samayai sammāsaṅkappau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai  tak‍kau vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā sammāsaṅkappau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāsaṅkappau hauti|

299. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sammāvācā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai catūhi vacīduc‍caritaihi ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau sammāvācā maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāvācā hauti|

300. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai sammākammantau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai tīhi kāyaduc‍caritaihi ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau sammākammantau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammākammantau hauti|

301. katamau tasmiṃ samayai sammāājīvau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai micchāājīvā ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau sammāājīvau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāājīvau hauti|

302. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai sammāvāyāmau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau  vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāvāyāmau hauti|

303. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai sammāsati hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāsati hauti|

304. katamau tasmiṃ samayai sammāsamādhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammāsamādhi hauti|

305. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saddhābalaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai saddhā saddahanā aukappanā abhippasādau saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saddhābalaṃ hauti|

306. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyabalaṃ hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyabalaṃ hauti|

307. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai satibalaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau  maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai satibalaṃ hauti|

308. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhibalaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhibalaṃ hauti|

309. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñābalaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau  pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñābalaṃ hauti|

310. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai hiribalaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai hirīyati hiriyitabbaina hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai hiribalaṃ hauti|

311. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai auttappabalaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai auttappati auttappitabbaina auttappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai auttappabalaṃ hauti|

312. katamau tasmiṃ samayai alaubhau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai alaubhau alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgau asāraj‍janā asāraj‍jitattaṃ anabhijjhā alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai alaubhau hauti|

313. katamau tasmiṃ samayai adausau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai adausau adussanā adussitattaṃ abyāpādau abyāpaj‍jau adausau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai adausau hauti|

314. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai amauhau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau  maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai amauhau hauti|

315. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai anabhijjhā hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai alaubhau alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgau asāraj‍janā asāraj‍jitattaṃ anabhijjhā alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai anabhijjhā hauti|

316. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai abyāpādau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai adausau adussanā adussitattaṃ abyāpādau abyāpaj‍jau adausau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai abyāpādau hauti|

317. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sammādiṭṭhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sammādiṭṭhi hauti|

318. katamā tasmiṃ samayai hirī hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai hirīyati hiriyitabbaina hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai hirī hauti|

319. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai auttappaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai auttappati auttappitabbaina auttappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai auttappaṃ hauti|

320. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai kāyapassaddhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhassa sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa passaddhi paṭipassaddhi passambhanā paṭipassambhanā paṭipassambhitattaṃ passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau  – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyapassaddhi hauti|

321. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittapassaddhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa  passaddhi paṭipassaddhi passambhanā paṭipassambhanā paṭipassambhitattaṃ passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittapassaddhi hauti|

322. katamā tasmiṃ samayai kāyalahutā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhassa sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa lahutā lahupariṇāmatā adandhanatā avitthanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyalahutā hauti|

323. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittalahutā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa lahutā lahupariṇāmatā  adandhanatā avitthanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittalahutā hauti|

324. katamā tasmiṃ samayai kāyamudutā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhassa sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa mudutā maddavatā akakkhaḷatā akathinatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyamudutā hauti|

325. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittamudutā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa mudutā maddavatā akakkhaḷatā akathinatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittamudutā hauti|

326. katamā tasmiṃ samayai kāyakammañ‍ñatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhassa sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa kammañ‍ñatā kammañ‍ñattaṃ  kammañ‍ñabhāvau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyakammañ‍ñatā hauti|

327. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittakammañ‍ñatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa kammañ‍ñatā kammañ‍ñattaṃ kammañ‍ñabhāvau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittakammañ‍ñatā hauti|

328. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai kāyapāguñ‍ñatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhassa sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa paguṇatā paguṇattaṃ paguṇabhāvau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyapāguñ‍ñatā hauti|

329. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai cittapāguñ‍ñatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa paguṇatā paguṇattaṃ paguṇabhāvau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittapāguñ‍ñatā hauti|

330. katamā tasmiṃ samayai kāyujukatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanākkhandhassa sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa ujutā ujukatā ajimhatā avaṅkatā akuṭilatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyujukatā hauti|

331. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittujukatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa ujutā ujukatā ajimhatā avaṅkatā akuṭilatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittujukatā hauti|

332. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sati hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ  maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sati hauti|

333. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai sampajañ‍ñaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sampajañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

334. katamau tasmiṃ samayai samathau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samathau hauti|

335. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai vipassanā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau  pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ  amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vipassanā hauti|

336. katamau tasmiṃ samayai paggāhau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai paggāhau hauti|

337. katamau tasmiṃ samayai avikkhaipau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai avikkhaipau hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, navindriyāni haunti, pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti, aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau hauti, satta balāni haunti, tayau haitū haunti, aikau phassau hauti, aikā vaidanā hauti, aikā sañ‍ñā hauti, aikā caitanā hauti, aikaṃ cittaṃ hauti, aikau vaidanākkhandhau  hauti, aikau sañ‍ñākkhandhau hauti, aikau saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti, aikau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau hauti, aikaṃ manāyatanaṃ hauti, aikaṃ manindriyaṃ hauti, aikā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti, aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā…pai॰…|

338. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau pīti  cittassaikaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ  jīvitindriyaṃ anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ hiribalaṃ auttappabalaṃ alaubhau adausau amauhau anabhijjhā abyāpādau sammādiṭṭhi hirī auttappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammañ‍ñatā cittakammañ‍ñatā kāyapāguñ‍ñatā cittapāguñ‍ñatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati sampajañ‍ñaṃ samathau vipassanā paggāhau avikkhaipau; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

339. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti  niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

340. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati sukhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

341. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

342. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā  vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā  …pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

suddhikapaṭipadā|

suñ‍ñataṃ

343. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti  niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

344. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitak‍kavicārānaṃ  vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

345. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

346. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

347. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ  diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati sukhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

348. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

349. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ  pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ  khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|
