appaṇihitaṃ

350. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati appaṇihitaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

351. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰…  catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati appaṇihitaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

appaṇihitaṃ|

appaṇihitamūlakapaṭipadā

352. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

353. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

354. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati sukhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

355. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

356. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ  diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitaṃ…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ  appaṇihitaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

appaṇihitamūlakapaṭipadā|

vīsati mahānayā

357. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ sammappadhānaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ indriyaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ balaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ baujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ sac‍caṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ samathaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ khandhaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ āyatanaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ dhātuṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ āhāraṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ phassaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ vaidanaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ sañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ caitanaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ  samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

vīsati mahānayā|

adhipati

358. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ chandādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… cittādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

359. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ  samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ  …pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ chandādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… cittādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

360. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ sammappadhānaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ indriyaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ balaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ baujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ  sac‍caṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ samathaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ khandhaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ āyatanaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ dhātuṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ āhāraṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ phassaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ vaidanaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ sañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ caitanaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰…  paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ chandādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… cittādhipataiyyaṃ…pai॰… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

adhipati|

paṭhamau maggau|

361. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ kāmarāgabyāpādānaṃ tanubhāvāya dutiyāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… añ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

dutiyau maggau|

362. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ kāmarāgabyāpādānaṃ anavasaisappahānāya tatiyāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… añ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā|

tatiyau maggau|

363. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ rūparāgaarūparāgamānauddhac‍caavij‍jāya anavasaisappahānāya catutthāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau  hauti…pai॰… añ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā…pai॰…|

364. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti? yā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ ñātānaṃ diṭṭhānaṃ pattānaṃ viditānaṃ sacchikatānaṃ sacchikiriyāya pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau  pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ, idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā kusalā|

catutthau maggau|

laukuttaraṃ cittaṃ|

dvādasa akusalāni

365. katamai  dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, pīti hauti, sukhaṃ hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, saumanassindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, micchādiṭṭhi hauti, micchāsaṅkappau hauti, micchāvāyāmau hauti, micchāsamādhi hauti, vīriyabalaṃ hauti, samādhibalaṃ  hauti, ahirikabalaṃ hauti, anauttappabalaṃ hauti, laubhau hauti, mauhau hauti, abhijjhā hauti, micchādiṭṭhi hauti, ahirikaṃ hauti, anauttappaṃ hauti, samathau hauti, paggāhau hauti, avikkhaipau hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā akusalā|

366. katamau tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti|

367. katamā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti|

368. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā sañ‍jānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti|

369. katamā tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā caitanā sañ‍caitanā caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti|

370. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ  paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti|

371. katamau tasmiṃ samayai vitak‍kau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai tak‍kau vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā micchāsaṅkappau  – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vitak‍kau hauti|

372. katamau tasmiṃ samayai vicārau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai cārau vicārau anuvicārau upavicārau cittassa anusandhānatā  anupaikkhanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vicārau hauti|

373. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai pīti hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pīti pāmauj‍jaṃ āmaudanā pamaudanā hāsau pahāsau vitti audagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pīti hauti|

374. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ hauti|

375. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ micchāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti|

376. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyindriyaṃ hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ micchāvāyāmau – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyindriyaṃ hauti|

377. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai samādhindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa  ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ micchāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhindriyaṃ hauti?

378. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti|

379. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saumanassindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saumanassindriyaṃ hauti|

380. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti? yau taisaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā  vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti|

381. katamā tasmiṃ samayai micchādiṭṭhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ diṭṭhigahanaṃ diṭṭhikantārau diṭṭhivisūkāyikaṃ diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ diṭṭhisaṃyaujanaṃ gāhau patiṭṭhāhau paṭiggāhau (sī॰ syā॰) abhinivaisau parāmāsau kummaggau micchāpathau micchattaṃ titthāyatanaṃ vipariyāsaggāhau vipariyaisagāhau (ka॰) – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai micchādiṭṭhi hauti|

382. katamau tasmiṃ samayai micchāsaṅkappau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai  tak‍kau vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā micchāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai micchāsaṅkappau hauti|

383. katamau tasmiṃ samayai micchāvāyāmau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā  anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ micchāvāyāmau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai micchāvāyāmau hauti|

384. katamau tasmiṃ samayai micchāsamādhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ micchāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai micchāsamādhi hauti|

385. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyabalaṃ hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ micchāvāyāmau – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyabalaṃ hauti|

386. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai samādhibalaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ micchāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ  samayai samādhibalaṃ hauti|

387. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai ahirikabalaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai na hirīyati hiriyitabbaina na hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai ahirikabalaṃ hauti|

388. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai anauttappabalaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai na auttappati auttappitabbaina na auttappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai anauttappabalaṃ hauti|

389. katamau tasmiṃ samayai laubhau hauti? yau  tasmiṃ samayai laubhau lubbhanā lubbhitattaṃ sārāgau sāraj‍janā sāraj‍jitattaṃ abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai laubhau hauti|

390. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai mauhau hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ anabhisamayau ananubaudhau asambaudhau appaṭivaidhau asaṃgāhanā apariyaugāhanā asamapaikkhanā apac‍cavaikkhanā apac‍cakkhakammaṃ dummaijjhaṃ bālyaṃ asampajañ‍ñaṃ mauhau pamauhau sammauhau avij‍jā avij‍jaughau avij‍jāyaugau avij‍jānusayau avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai mauhau hauti|

391. katamā tasmiṃ samayai abhijjhā hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai laubhau lubbhanā lubbhitattaṃ sārāgau sāraj‍janā sāraj‍jitattaṃ abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai abhijjhā hauti|

392. katamā tasmiṃ samayai micchādiṭṭhi hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai  diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ diṭṭhigahanaṃ diṭṭhikantārau diṭṭhivisūkāyikaṃ diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ diṭṭhisaṃyaujanaṃ gāhau patiṭṭhāhau abhinivaisau parāmāsau kummaggau micchāpathau  micchattaṃ titthāyatanaṃ vipariyāsaggāhau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai micchādiṭṭhi hauti|

393. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai ahirikaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai na hirīyati hiriyitabbaina na hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai ahirikaṃ hauti|

394. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai anauttappaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai na auttappati auttappitabbaina na auttappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai anauttappaṃ hauti|

395. katamau tasmiṃ samayai samathau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ micchāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samathau hauti|

396. katamau tasmiṃ samayai paggāhau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau  nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ micchāvāyāmau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai paggāhau hauti|

397. katamau tasmiṃ samayai avikkhaipau hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai  cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ micchāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai avikkhaipau hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā akusalā|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, pañ‍cindriyāni haunti, pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti, caturaṅgikau maggau hauti, cattāri balāni haunti, dvai haitū haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ  hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā akusalā…pai॰…|

398. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau pīti cittassaikaggatā vīriyindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ micchādiṭṭhi micchāsaṅkappau micchāvāyāmau micchāsamādhi vīriyabalaṃ samādhibalaṃ ahirikabalaṃ anauttappabalaṃ laubhau mauhau abhijjhā micchādiṭṭhi ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ samathau paggāhau avikkhaipau; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai  dhammā akusalā|

399. katamai dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā akusalā|

400. katamai  dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, pīti hauti, sukhaṃ hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, saumanassindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, micchāsaṅkappau hauti, micchāvāyāmau hauti, micchāsamādhi hauti, vīriyabalaṃ hauti, samādhibalaṃ hauti, ahirikabalaṃ hauti, anauttappabalaṃ hauti, laubhau hauti, mauhau hauti, abhijjhā hauti, ahirikaṃ hauti, anauttappaṃ hauti, samathau hauti, paggāhau hauti, avikkhaipau hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā akusalā…pai॰…|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai  cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, pañ‍cindriyāni haunti, pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti, tivaṅgikau maggau hauti, cattāri balāni haunti, dvai haitū haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ  hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā akusalā…pai॰…|

404. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti|

405. katamā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ  vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti…pai॰…|

406. katamā tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ  vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhā hauti…pai॰…|

407. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā akusalā|

419. katamau tasmiṃ samayai byāpādau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai dausau dussanā dussitattaṃ byāpatti byāpaj‍janā byāpaj‍jitattaṃ viraudhau paṭiviraudhau caṇḍik‍kaṃ asuraupau anattamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai byāpādau hauti…pai॰… yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā akusalā|

420. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau cittassaikaggatā vīriyindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ micchāsaṅkappau micchāvāyāmau  micchāsamādhi vīriyabalaṃ samādhibalaṃ ahirikabalaṃ anauttappabalaṃ dausau mauhau byāpādau ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ samathau paggāhau avikkhaipau; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā akusalā|

421. katamai  dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti daumanassasahagataṃ paṭighasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā akusalā|

422. katamai dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ vicikicchāsampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ  hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, micchāsaṅkappau hauti, micchāvāyāmau hauti, vīriyabalaṃ hauti, ahirikabalaṃ hauti, anauttappabalaṃ hauti, vicikicchā hauti, mauhau hauti, ahirikaṃ hauti, anauttappaṃ hauti, paggāhau hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā akusalā|

423. katamau tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰…|

424. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti…pai॰…|

425. katamā tasmiṃ samayai vicikicchā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai kaṅkhā kaṅkhāyanā kaṅkhāyitattaṃ vimati vicikicchā dvaiḷhakaṃ dvaidhāpathau saṃsayau anaikaṃsaggāhau āsappanā parisappanā apariyaugāhanā thambhitattaṃ cittassa manauvilaikhau – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vicikicchā hauti…pai॰…  yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā akusalā|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, cattāri indriyāni haunti, caturaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti, duvaṅgikau maggau hauti, tīṇi balāni haunti, aikau haitu hauti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā akusalā…pai॰…|

426. katamau tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau cittassaikaggatā vīriyindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ micchāsaṅkappau micchāvāyāmau vīriyabalaṃ ahirikabalaṃ anauttappabalaṃ vicikicchā mauhau ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ paggāhau; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā akusalā|

427. katamai dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, micchāsaṅkappau hauti, micchāvāyāmau hauti, micchāsamādhi hauti, vīriyabalaṃ hauti, samādhibalaṃ hauti, ahirikabalaṃ hauti, anauttappabalaṃ hauti, uddhac‍caṃ hauti, mauhau hauti, ahirikaṃ hauti, anauttappaṃ hauti, samathau hauti, paggāhau hauti, avikkhaipau hauti; yai  vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā akusalā|

428. katamau tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰…|

429. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai uddhac‍caṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittassa uddhac‍caṃ avūpasamau caitasau vikkhaipau bhantattaṃ cittassa – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai  uddhac‍caṃ hauti  …pai॰… yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā akusalā|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, pañ‍cindriyāni haunti, caturaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti, tivaṅgikau maggau hauti, cattāri balāni haunti, aikau haitu hauti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā akusalā…pai॰…|

430. katamau tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau cittassaikaggatā vīriyindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ micchāsaṅkappau micchāvāyāmau micchāsamādhi vīriyabalaṃ samādhibalaṃ ahirikabalaṃ anauttappabalaṃ uddhac‍caṃ mauhau ahirikaṃ anauttappaṃ samathau  paggāhau avikkhaipau; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā akusalā|

dvādasa akusalacittāni|

abyākatavipākau

kusalavipākapañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇāni

431. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

432. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti|

433. katamā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jācakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti|

434. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jācakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā sañ‍jānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti|

435. katamā tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jācakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā caitanā sañ‍caitanā caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti|

436. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jācakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti|

437. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhā hauti|

438. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti|

439. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jācakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti|

440. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai  caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti|

441. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti? yau taisaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, tīṇindriyāni haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti, aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā  dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰…|

442. katamau tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā cittassaikaggatā jīvitindriyaṃ; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā  vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

443. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa  kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ saddārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ gandhārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rasārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti sukhasahagataṃ phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, sukhaṃ hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, sukhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

444. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti|

445. katamā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jākāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ kāyikaṃ sātaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti|

446. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jākāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā sañ‍jānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti|

447. katamā tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jākāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā caitanā sañ‍caitanā caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti|

448. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jākāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti|

449. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyikaṃ sātaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ hauti|

450. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti|

451. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jākāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti|

452. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai sukhindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyikaṃ sātaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sukhindriyaṃ hauti|

453. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti? yau taisaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti; yai  vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, tīṇindriyāni haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti, aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰…|

454. katamau tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā cittassaikaggatā jīvitindriyaṃ; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā  vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

kusalavipākāni pañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇāni|

kusalavipākamanaudhātu

455. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manaudhātu uppan‍nā hauti upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

456. katamau tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti|

457. katamā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanaudhātusamphassajaṃ caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti|

458. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanaudhātusamphassajā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā sañ‍jānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti|

459. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanaudhātusamphassajā caitanā sañ‍caitanā caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti|

460. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanaudhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti|

461. katamau tasmiṃ samayai vitak‍kau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai tak‍kau vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā – ayaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai vitak‍kau hauti|

462. katamau tasmiṃ samayai vicārau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai cārau vicārau anuvicārau upavicārau cittassa anusandhānatā anupaikkhanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vicārau hauti|

463. katamā  tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhā hauti? yaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhā hauti|

464. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti|

465. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanaudhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti|

466. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti|

467. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti? yau taisaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti; yai  vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, tīṇindriyāni haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikā manaudhātu hauti, aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰…|

468. katamau tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau cittassaikaggatā jīvitindriyaṃ; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ  ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

kusalavipākā manaudhātu|

kusalavipākamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusaumanassasahagatā

469. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti saumanassasahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā  vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, pīti hauti, sukhaṃ hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ  hauti, saumanassindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

470. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti|

471. katamā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti|

472. katamā tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā sañ‍jānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sañ‍ñā hauti|

473. katamā tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti| yā tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā caitanā sañ‍caitanā caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitanā hauti|

474. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti|

475. katamau tasmiṃ samayai vitak‍kau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai tak‍kau  vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vitak‍kau hauti|

476. katamau tasmiṃ samayai vicārau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai cārau vicārau anuvicārau upavicārau cittassa anusandhānatā anupaikkhanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vicārau hauti|

477. katamā tasmiṃ samayai pīti hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai pīti pāmauj‍jaṃ āmaudanā pamaudanā hāsau pahāsau vitti audagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai pīti hauti|

478. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai sukhaṃ hauti|

479. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti|

480. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai manindriyaṃ hauti|

481. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saumanassindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ  caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saumanassindriyaṃ hauti|

482. katamaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti? yau taisaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai jīvitindriyaṃ hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, tīṇindriyāni haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti, aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰…|

483. katamau tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau pīti cittassaikaggatā jīvitindriyaṃ; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā  arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

498. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti saumanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhāraina  rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti  …pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰… alaubhau abyākatamūlaṃ…pai॰… adausau abyākatamūlaṃ…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

rūpāvacaravipākā

499. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰…  imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

500. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ  samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

arūpāvacaravipākā

501. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai  dhammā kusalā| tassaiva arūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa  ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

502. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva arūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

503. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva arūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa  katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ  sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

504. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau  ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva arūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

arūpāvacaravipākā|

laukuttaravipāka-paṭhamamaggavipākā

suddhikapaṭipadā

505. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau  hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… añ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

506. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā  kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ animittaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… añ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

507. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā  pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… añ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

508. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñanti kusalaṃ…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñatanti vipākau…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñanti kusalaṃ…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ animittanti vipākau…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñanti kusalaṃ…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitanti vipākau, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

509. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñanti kusalaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ  khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñatanti vipākau…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñanti kusalaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ  animittanti  vipākau…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñanti kusalaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ  khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitanti vipākau, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

suddhikapaṭipadā|

526. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitanti kusalaṃ…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitanti vipākau…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ  dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitanti kusalaṃ…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ animittanti vipākau…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitanti kusalaṃ…pai॰… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñatanti vipākau, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

527. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ  khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitanti kusalaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitanti vipākau…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitanti kusalaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ animittanti vipākau…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ appaṇihitanti kusalaṃ…pai॰… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñatanti vipākau, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

appaṇihitapaṭipadā|

vīsati mahānayā

528. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ maggaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ sammappadhānaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰…  laukuttaraṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ indriyaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ balaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ baujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ sac‍caṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ samathaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ khandhaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ āyatanaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ dhātuṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ āhāraṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ phassaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ vaidanaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ sañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ caitanaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti  …pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ…pai॰… animittaṃ  …pai॰… appaṇihitaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

paṭhamamaggavipākau|

dutiyādimaggavipākau

553. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ kāmarāgabyāpādānaṃ  tanubhāvāya dutiyāya  bhūmiyā pattiyā…pai॰… kāmarā gabyāpādānaṃ  anavasaisappahānāya tatiyāya bhūmiyā pattiyā…pai॰… rūparāgaarūparāgamānauddhac‍caavij‍jāya anavasaisappahānāya catutthāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… añ‍ñindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

554. katamau tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰…|

555. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ hauti? yā taisaṃ añ‍ñātāvīnaṃ dhammānaṃ añ‍ñā pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ  amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

dutiyādimaggavipākau|

laukuttaravipākau|

akusalavipākaabyākataṃ

akusalavipākapañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇāni

556. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ saddārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ gandhārammaṇaṃ  …pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rasārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti dukkhasahagataṃ phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, dukkhaṃ hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, dukkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

557. katamau tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti|

558. katamā tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai taj‍jākāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ  kāyikaṃ asātaṃ kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vaidanā hauti…pai॰…|

559. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai dukkhaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyikaṃ asātaṃ kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai dukkhaṃ hauti…pai॰…|

560. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai dukkhindriyaṃ hauti? yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai kāyikaṃ asātaṃ kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā  – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai dukkhindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

tasmiṃ  khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, tīṇindriyāni haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti, aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰…|

561. katamau tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā cittassaikaggatā jīvitindriyaṃ; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau  hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

akusalavipākapañ‍caviñ‍ñāṇāni|

akusalavipākamanaudhātu

562. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manaudhātu uppan‍nā hauti upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰…|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau  āhārā haunti, tīṇindriyāni haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikā manaudhātu hauti, aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰…|

563. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau cittassaikaggatā jīvitindriyaṃ; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ  – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

akusalavipākā manaudhātu|

akusalavipākamanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, tīṇindriyāni haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti, aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰…|

565. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau cittassaikaggatā jīvitindriyaṃ; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ  ṭhapaitvā  sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

kiriyāmanaudhātu

566. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai manaudhātu uppan‍nā hauti kiriyā naiva kusalā nākusalā na ca kammavipākā upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰…|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, tīṇindriyāni haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikā manaudhātu hauti, aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰…|

567. katamau tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau  vicārau cittassaikaggatā  jīvitindriyaṃ; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

kiriyā manaudhātu|

kiriyāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusaumanassasahagatā

568. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti kiriyā naiva  kusalā nākusalā na ca kammavipākā saumanassasahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, pīti hauti, sukhaṃ hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, saumanassindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

569. katamau tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰…|

570. katamā tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittassaikaggatā hauti|

571. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyindriyaṃ hauti? yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau  vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā  anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai vīriyindriyaṃ hauti|

572. katamaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhindriyaṃ hauti? yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samayai samādhindriyaṃ hauti…pai॰… yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, pañ‍cindriyāni haunti, aikau  phassau hauti…pai॰… aikā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti, aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰…|

573. katamau tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau pīti cittassaikaggatā vīriyindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā  vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

kiriyā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu saumanassasahagatā|

kiriyāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuupaikkhāsahagatā

574. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti kiriyā naiva kusalā nākusalā na ca kammavipākā upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā  hauti, vīriyindriyaṃ hauti, samādhindriyaṃ hauti manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰…|

tasmiṃ khau pana samayai cattārau khandhā haunti, dvāyatanāni haunti, dvai dhātuyau haunti, tayau āhārā haunti, pañ‍cindriyāni haunti, aikau phassau hauti…pai॰… aikā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu hauti, aikaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hauti, aikā dhammadhātu hauti; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā – imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰…|

575. katamau  tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti? phassau caitanā vitak‍kau vicārau cittassaikaggatā vīriyindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ; yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā ṭhapaitvā vaidanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ ṭhapaitvā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārakkhandhau hauti…pai॰… imai  dhammā abyākatā|

kiriyā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu upaikkhāsahagatā|

ahaitukā kiriyā abyākatā|

sahaitukakāmāvacarakiriyā

576. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti kiriyā naiva kusalā nākusalā na ca kammavipākā saumanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti  …pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰… alaubhau abyākatamūlaṃ…pai॰… adausau abyākatamūlaṃ…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

sahaitukā kāmāvacarakiriyā|

rūpāvacarakiriyā

577. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai rūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti kiriyaṃ naiva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ  samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

578. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai rūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti kiriyaṃ naiva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

rūpāvacarakiriyā|

arūpāvacarakiriyā

579. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti kiriyaṃ naiva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ  sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

580. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti kiriyaṃ naiva  kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau  hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

581. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti kiriyaṃ naiva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

582. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti kiriyaṃ naiva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā…pai॰… alaubhau abyākatamūlaṃ…pai॰… adausau abyākatamūlaṃ…pai॰… amauhau abyākatamūlaṃ…pai॰… imai dhammā abyākatā|

arūpāvacarakiriyā|

kiriyā abyākatā|

cittuppādakaṇḍaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. rūpakaṇḍaṃ

uddaisau

583. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? kusalākusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ vipākā kāmāvacarā rūpāvacarā arūpāvacarā apariyāpan‍nā vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau, yai ca dhammā kiriyā naiva kusalā nākusalā na ca kammavipākā, sabbañ‍ca rūpaṃ, asaṅkhatā ca dhātu – imai dhammā abyākatā|

mātikā

aikakaṃ

584. tattha katamaṃ sabbaṃ rūpaṃ? cattārau ca mahābhūtā, catun‍nañ‍ca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya rūpaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati sabbaṃ rūpaṃ| sabbaṃ rūpaṃ na haitu, ahaitukaṃ, haituvippayuttaṃ, sappac‍cayaṃ, saṅkhataṃ, rūpaṃ rūpiyaṃ (sī॰), laukiyaṃ, sāsavaṃ, saṃyaujaniyaṃ, ganthaniyaṃ, aughaniyaṃ, yauganiyaṃ, nīvaraṇiyaṃ, parāmaṭṭhaṃ, upādāniyaṃ, saṃkilaisikaṃ, abyākataṃ, anārammaṇaṃ, acaitasikaṃ, cittavippayuttaṃ, naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ, asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ, na savitak‍kasavicāraṃ, na avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ, avitak‍kaavicāraṃ, na pītisahagataṃ, na sukhasahagataṃ, na upaikkhāsahagataṃ, naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ, naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ, naiva ācayagāmi na apacayagāmi, naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ, parittaṃ, kāmāvacaraṃ, na rūpāvacaraṃ, na  arūpāvacaraṃ, pariyāpan‍naṃ, nau apariyāpan‍naṃ, aniyataṃ, aniyyānikaṃ, uppan‍naṃ, chahi viñ‍ñāṇaihi viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, anic‍caṃ, jarābhibhūtaṃ|

aivaṃ aikavidhaina rūpasaṅgahau|

aikakaṃ|

dukaṃ

duvidhaina  rūpasaṅgahau –

atthi rūpaṃ upādā, atthi rūpaṃ nau upādā|

atthi rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ sanidassanaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ anidassanaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ sappaṭighaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ appaṭighaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ indriyaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ na indriyaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ na mahābhūtaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ viñ‍ñatti, atthi rūpaṃ na viñ‍ñatti|

atthi rūpaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ na cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ cittasahabhu, atthi rūpaṃ na cittasahabhu|

atthi rūpaṃ cittānuparivatti, atthi rūpaṃ na cittānuparivatti|

atthi rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ auḷārikaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ sukhumaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ dūrai, atthi rūpaṃ santikai|

atthi rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassassa vatthu, atthi rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassassa na vatthu| atthi rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassajāya  vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa vatthu, atthi rūpaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu|

atthi rūpaṃ sautasamphassassa…pai॰… ghānasamphassassa…pai॰…  jivhāsamphassassa…pai॰… kāyasamphassassa vatthu, atthi rūpaṃ kāyasamphassassa na vatthu| atthi rūpaṃ kāyasamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya  …pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa vatthu, atthi rūpaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu|

atthi rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassassa ārammaṇaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassassa nārammaṇaṃ| atthi rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa nārammaṇaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ sautasamphassassa…pai॰…  ghānasamphassassa…pai॰… jivhāsamphassassa…pai॰… kāyasamphassassa ārammaṇaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ kāyasamphassassa nārammaṇaṃ| atthi rūpaṃ kāyasamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa nārammaṇaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ na cakkhāyatanaṃ| atthi rūpaṃ sautāyatanaṃ…pai॰… ghānāyatanaṃ…pai॰… jivhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ na kāyāyatanaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ na rūpāyatanaṃ| atthi rūpaṃ saddāyatanaṃ…pai॰… gandhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ na phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ cakkhudhātu, atthi rūpaṃ na cakkhudhātu| atthi rūpaṃ sautadhātu…pai॰… ghānadhātu…pai॰… jivhādhātu…pai॰… kāyadhātu, atthi rūpaṃ na kāyadhātu|

atthi rūpaṃ rūpadhātu, atthi rūpaṃ na rūpadhātu| atthi rūpaṃ saddadhātu…pai॰… gandhadhātu…pai॰… rasadhātu…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbadhātu, atthi rūpaṃ na phauṭṭhabbadhātu|

atthi  rūpaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ na cakkhundriyaṃ| atthi rūpaṃ sautindriyaṃ…pai॰… ghānindriyaṃ…pai॰… jivhindriyaṃ…pai॰… kāyindriyaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ na kāyindriyaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ itthindriyaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ na itthindriyaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ purisindriyaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ na purisindriyaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ na jīvitindriyaṃ|

atthi rūpaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti, atthi rūpaṃ na kāyaviñ‍ñatti|

atthi rūpaṃ vacīviñ‍ñatti, atthi rūpaṃ na vacīviñ‍ñatti|

atthi rūpaṃ ākāsadhātu, atthi rūpaṃ na ākāsadhātu|

atthi rūpaṃ  āpaudhātu, atthi rūpaṃ na āpaudhātu|

atthi rūpaṃ rūpassa lahutā, atthi rūpaṃ rūpassa na lahutā|

atthi rūpaṃ rūpassa mudutā, atthi rūpaṃ  rūpassa na mudutā|

atthi rūpaṃ rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā, atthi rūpaṃ rūpassa na kammañ‍ñatā|

atthi rūpaṃ rūpassa upacayau, atthi rūpaṃ rūpassa na upacayau|

atthi rūpaṃ rūpassa santati, atthi rūpaṃ rūpassa na santati|

atthi  rūpaṃ rūpassa jaratā, atthi rūpaṃ rūpassa na jaratā|

atthi rūpaṃ rūpassa anic‍catā, atthi rūpaṃ rūpassa na anic‍catā|

dukaṃ|

tikaṃ

tividhaina  rūpasaṅgahau –

585. yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ upādā| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi upādā, atthi nau upādā|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ upādiṇṇaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi upādiṇṇaṃ, atthi anupādiṇṇaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ, atthi anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ anidassanaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi sanidassanaṃ, atthi anidassanaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ sappaṭighaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi sappaṭighaṃ, atthi appaṭighaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ na mahābhūtaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi mahābhūtaṃ, atthi na mahābhūtaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ na viñ‍ñatti| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi viñ‍ñatti, atthi na viñ‍ñatti|

yaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ na cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ, atthi na cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ na cittasahabhu| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi  cittasahabhu, atthi na cittasahabhu|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ na cittānuparivatti| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi cittānuparivatti, atthi na cittānuparivatti|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ santikai| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi dūrai, atthi santikai|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ cakkhusamphassassa na vatthu| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ  atthi cakkhusamphassassa vatthu, atthi cakkhusamphassassa na vatthu|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ cakkhusamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ atthi cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa vatthu, atthi cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ sautasamphassassa…pai॰… ghānasamphassassa…pai॰… jivhāsamphassassa…pai॰… kāyasamphassassa na vatthu| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ atthi kāyasamphassassa vatthu, atthi kāyasamphassassa na vatthu| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ kāyasamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ atthi kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa vatthu, atthi kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu|

yaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ cakkhusamphassassa nārammaṇaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi cakkhusamphassassa ārammaṇaṃ, atthi cakkhusamphassassa nārammaṇaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ cakkhusamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa nārammaṇaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi  cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ, atthi cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa nārammaṇaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ sautasamphassassa…pai॰… ghānasamphassassa…pai॰… jivhāsamphassassa…pai॰… kāyasamphassassa nārammaṇaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi kāyasamphassassa ārammaṇaṃ, atthi kāyasamphassassa nārammaṇaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ kāyasamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa nārammaṇaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ, atthi kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa nārammaṇaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ na cakkhāyatanaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ atthi cakkhāyatanaṃ, atthi na cakkhāyatanaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ na sautāyatanaṃ…pai॰… na ghānāyatanaṃ…pai॰… na jivhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… na kāyāyatanaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ atthi kāyāyatanaṃ, atthi na kāyāyatanaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ na rūpāyatanaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi rūpāyatanaṃ, atthi na rūpāyatanaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ na saddāyatanaṃ…pai॰… na gandhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… na rasāyatanaṃ…pai॰… na phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, atthi na phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ na cakkhudhātu| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ atthi cakkhudhātu, atthi na cakkhudhātu| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ na sautadhātu…pai॰… na ghānadhātu…pai॰… na jivhādhātu…pai॰… na kāyadhātu| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ atthi kāyadhātu, atthi na kāyadhātu|

yaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ na rūpadhātu| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi rūpadhātu, atthi na rūpadhātu| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ na saddadhātu…pai॰… na gandhadhātu…pai॰… na  rasadhātu…pai॰… na phauṭṭhabbadhātu| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ atthi phauṭṭhabbadhātu, atthi na phauṭṭhabbadhātu|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ na cakkhundriyaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ atthi cakkhundriyaṃ, atthi na cakkhundriyaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ, taṃ na sautindriyaṃ…pai॰… na ghānindriyaṃ…pai॰… na jivhindriyaṃ…pai॰… na kāyindriyaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ, taṃ atthi kāyindriyaṃ, atthi na kāyindriyaṃ|

586. yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā, taṃ atthi upādiṇṇaṃ, atthi anupādiṇṇaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ nau upādā, taṃ atthi upādiṇṇaṃ, atthi anupādiṇṇaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ, taṃ atthi sappaṭighaṃ atthi appaṭighaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ, taṃ atthi sappaṭighaṃ, atthi appaṭighaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ, taṃ atthi mahābhūtaṃ, atthi  na mahābhūtaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ, taṃ atthi mahābhūtaṃ, atthi na mahābhūtaṃ|

yaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ, taṃ atthi auḷārikaṃ, atthi sukhumaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ, taṃ atthi auḷārikaṃ, atthi sukhumaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ, taṃ atthi sappaṭighaṃ, atthi appaṭighaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ, taṃ atthi sappaṭighaṃ, atthi appaṭighaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ, taṃ atthi mahābhūtaṃ, atthi na mahābhūtaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ, taṃ atthi mahābhūtaṃ, atthi na mahābhūtaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ, taṃ atthi auḷārikaṃ, atthi sukhumaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ, taṃ atthi auḷārikaṃ, atthi sukhumaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ, taṃ atthi dūrai, atthi santikai| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ, taṃ atthi dūrai, atthi santikai|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sappaṭighaṃ, taṃ atthi indriyaṃ, atthi na indriyaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ appaṭighaṃ, taṃ atthi indriyaṃ, atthi na indriyaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ indriyaṃ, taṃ atthi auḷārikaṃ, atthi sukhumaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na indriyaṃ, taṃ atthi auḷārikaṃ, atthi sukhumaṃ|

yaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ indriyaṃ, taṃ atthi dūrai, atthi santikai| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na indriyaṃ, taṃ atthi dūrai, atthi santikai|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, taṃ atthi auḷārikaṃ, atthi sukhumaṃ| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na mahābhūtaṃ, taṃ atthi auḷārikaṃ, atthi sukhumaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ mahābhūtaṃ, taṃ atthi dūrai, atthi  santikai| yaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na mahābhūtaṃ, taṃ atthi dūrai, atthi santikai|

diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ rūpaṃ|
