594. sabbaṃ  rūpaṃ na haitumaiva, ahaitukamaiva, haituvippayuttamaiva, sappac‍cayamaiva, saṅkhatamaiva, rūpamaiva, laukiyamaiva, sāsavamaiva, saṃyaujaniyamaiva, ganthaniyamaiva, aughaniyamaiva, yauganiyamaiva, nīvaraṇiyamaiva, parāmaṭṭhamaiva, upādāniyamaiva, saṃkilaisikamaiva, abyākatamaiva, anārammaṇamaiva, acaitasikamaiva, cittavippayuttamaiva, naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammamaiva, asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikamaiva, na savitak‍kasavicāramaiva, na avitak‍kavicāramattamaiva, avitak‍kaavicāramaiva, na pītisahagatamaiva, na sukhasahagatamaiva, na upaikkhāsahagatamaiva, naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbamaiva, naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukamaiva, naiva ācayagāmi na apacayagāmimaiva, naivasaikkhanāsaikkhamaiva, parittamaiva, kāmāvacaramaiva, na rūpāvacaramaiva, na arūpāvacaramaiva, pariyāpan‍namaiva, nau apariyāpan‍namaiva, aniyatamaiva, aniyyānikamaiva, uppan‍naṃ chahi viñ‍ñāṇaihi viñ‍ñaiyyamaiva, anic‍camaiva, jarābhibhūtamaiva|

aivaṃ aikavidhaina rūpasaṅgahau|

aikakaniddaisau|

dukaniddaisau

upādābhājanīyaṃ

595. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ upādā? cakkhāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, itthindriyaṃ, purisindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñatti, vacīviñ‍ñatti, ākāsadhātu, rūpassa lahutā, rūpassa mudutā, rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā, rūpassa upacayau, rūpassa santati, rūpassa jaratā, rūpassa anic‍catā, kabaḷīkārau āhārau|

596. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cakkhu cakkhuṃ (sī॰ syā॰) catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaina cakkhunā anidassanaina sappaṭighaina rūpaṃ sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ passi vā passati vā passissati vā passai vā, cakkhuṃ paitaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ paitaṃ cakkhudhātu paisā cakkhundriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ naittaṃ paitaṃ nayanaṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ  tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmaupaisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ|

597. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cakkhu catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yamhi cakkhumhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi rūpaṃ sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, cakkhuṃ paitaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ paitaṃ cakkhudhātu paisā cakkhundriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ naittaṃ paitaṃ nayanaṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ|

598. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cakkhu catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ cakkhu anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ rūpamhi sanidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, cakkhuṃ paitaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ paitaṃ cakkhudhātu paisā cakkhundriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā  samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ naittaṃ paitaṃ nayanaṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ|

599. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cakkhu catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ cakkhuṃ  nissāya rūpaṃ ārabbha cakkhusamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ cakkhuṃ nissāya rūpaṃ ārabbha cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ  uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ cakkhuṃ nissāya rūpārammaṇau cakkhusamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ cakkhuṃ nissāya rūpārammaṇā cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ  uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā, cakkhuṃ paitaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ paitaṃ cakkhudhātu paisā cakkhundriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ naittaṃ paitaṃ nayanaṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ|

600. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sautāyatanaṃ? yaṃ sautaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaina sautaina anidassanaina sappaṭighaina saddaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ suṇi vā suṇāti vā suṇissati vā suṇai vā, sautaṃ paitaṃ sautāyatanaṃ paitaṃ sautadhātu paisā sautindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sautāyatanaṃ|

601. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sautāyatanaṃ? yaṃ sautaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yamhi sautamhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi saddau anidassanau sappaṭighau paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, sautaṃ paitaṃ sautāyatanaṃ paitaṃ sautadhātu paisā sautindriyaṃ paitaṃ  laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sautāyatanaṃ|

602. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ sautāyatanaṃ? yaṃ sautaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ sautaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ saddamhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, sautaṃ paitaṃ sautāyatanaṃ paitaṃ sautadhātu paisā sautindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sautāyatanaṃ|

603. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sautāyatanaṃ? yaṃ  sautaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ sautaṃ nissāya saddaṃ ārabbha sautasamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati  vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ sautaṃ nissāya saddaṃ ārabbha sautasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ sautaṃ nissāya saddārammaṇau sautasamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ sautaṃ nissāya saddārammaṇā sautasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā, sautaṃ paitaṃ sautāyatanaṃ paitaṃ sautadhātu paisā sautindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau  paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sautāyatanaṃ|

604. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ? yaṃ ghānaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaina ghānaina anidassanaina sappaṭighaina gandhaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ ghāyi vā ghāyati vā ghāyissati vā ghāyai vā, ghānaṃ paitaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ paitaṃ ghānadhātu paisā ghānindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ|

605. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ? yaṃ ghānaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yamhi ghānamhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi gandhau anidassanau sappaṭighau paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, ghānaṃ  paitaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ paitaṃ ghānadhātu paisā ghānindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ|

606. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ? yaṃ ghānaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ ghānaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ gandhamhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, ghānaṃ paitaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ paitaṃ  ghānadhātu paisā ghānindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau  paisau  paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmaupaisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ|

607. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ? yaṃ ghānaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ ghānaṃ nissāya gandhaṃ ārabbha ghānasamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ ghānaṃ nissāya gandhaṃ ārabbha ghānasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ ghānaṃ nissāya gandhārammaṇau ghānasamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ ghānaṃ nissāya gandhārammaṇā ghānasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā, ghānaṃ paitaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ paitaṃ ghānadhātu paisā ghānindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ|

608. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ jivhāyatanaṃ? yā jivhā catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yāya jivhāya anidassanāya sappaṭighāya rasaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ sāyi vā sāyati vā sāyissati vā sāyai vā, jivhā paisā jivhāyatanaṃ paitaṃ jivhādhātu paisā jivhindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau  paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ jivhāyatanaṃ|

609. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ jivhāyatanaṃ? yā jivhā catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yāya jivhāya anidassanāya sappaṭighāya rasau anidassanau sappaṭighau paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, jivhā paisā jivhāyatanaṃ paitaṃ jivhādhātu paisā jivhindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ jivhāyatanaṃ|

610. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ jivhāyatanaṃ? yā jivhā catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yā  jivhā anidassanā sappaṭighā rasamhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, jivhā paisā jivhāyatanaṃ paitaṃ jivhādhātu paisā jivhindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ jivhāyatanaṃ|

611. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ jivhāyatanaṃ? yā jivhā catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ jivhaṃ nissāya rasaṃ ārabbha jivhāsamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ jivhaṃ nissāya rasaṃ ārabbha jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ  uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ jivhaṃ nissāya rasārammaṇau jivhāsamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ jivhaṃ nissāya rasārammaṇā jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā, jivhā paisā jivhāyatanaṃ paitaṃ jivhādhātu paisā jivhindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ jivhāyatanaṃ|

612. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ? yau kāyau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaina kāyaina anidassanaina sappaṭighaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ anidassanasappaṭighaṃ  phusi vā phusati vā phusissati vā phusai vā, kāyau paisau kāyāyatanaṃ paitaṃ kāyadhātu paisā kāyindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ|

613. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ? yau kāyau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yamhi kāyamhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi phauṭṭhabbau anidassanau sappaṭighau paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati  vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, kāyau paisau kāyāyatanaṃ paitaṃ kāyadhātu paisā kāyindriyaṃ  paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ  vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ|

614. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ? yau kāyau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yau kāyau anidassanau sappaṭighau phauṭṭhabbamhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, kāyau paisau kāyāyatanaṃ paitaṃ kāyadhātu paisā kāyindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ|

615. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ? yau kāyau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ kāyaṃ nissāya phauṭṭhabbaṃ ārabbha kāyasamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ kāyaṃ nissāya phauṭṭhabbaṃ ārabbha kāyasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ kāyaṃ nissāya phauṭṭhabbārammaṇau kāyasamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ kāyaṃ nissāya phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā kāyasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati  vā uppaj‍jai vā, kāyau paisau kāyāyatanaṃ paitaṃ kāyadhātu paisā kāyindriyaṃ paitaṃ laukau paisau dvārā  paisā samuddau paisau paṇḍaraṃ paitaṃ khaittaṃ paitaṃ vatthuṃ paitaṃ aurimaṃ tīraṃ paitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ|

616. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ nīlaṃ pītakaṃ lauhitakaṃ audātaṃ kāḷakaṃ mañ‍jiṭṭhakaṃ mañ‍jaiṭṭhakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) hari harivaṇṇaṃ ambaṅkuravaṇṇaṃ dīghaṃ rassaṃ aṇuṃ thūlaṃ vaṭṭaṃ parimaṇḍalaṃ caturaṃsaṃ caturassaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) chaḷaṃsaṃ aṭṭhaṃsaṃ sauḷasaṃsaṃ nin‍naṃ thalaṃ chāyā ātapau ālaukau andhakārau abbhā mahikā dhūmau rajau candamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā sūriyamaṇḍalassa suriyamaṇḍalassa (sī॰ syā॰) vaṇṇanibhā tārakarūpānaṃ  vaṇṇanibhā ādāsamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā maṇisaṅkhamuttāvaiḷuriyassa vaṇṇanibhā jātarūparajatassa vaṇṇanibhā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ, yaṃ rūpaṃ sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ cakkhunā anidassanaina sappaṭighaina passi vā passati vā passissati vā passai vā, rūpaṃ paitaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ paitaṃ rūpadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ|

617. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ nīlaṃ pītakaṃ lauhitakaṃ audātaṃ kāḷakaṃ mañ‍jiṭṭhakaṃ hari harivaṇṇaṃ ambaṅkuravaṇṇaṃ dīghaṃ rassaṃ aṇuṃ thūlaṃ vaṭṭaṃ parimaṇḍalaṃ caturaṃsaṃ chaḷaṃsaṃ aṭṭhaṃsaṃ sauḷasaṃsaṃ nin‍naṃ thalaṃ chāyā ātapau ālaukau andhakārau  abbhā mahikā dhūmau rajau candamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā sūriyamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā tārakarūpānaṃ vaṇṇanibhā ādāsamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā maṇisaṅkhamuttāvaiḷuriyassa vaṇṇanibhā jātarūparajatassa vaṇṇanibhā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ, yamhi rūpamhi sanidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi cakkhuṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, rūpaṃ paitaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ paitaṃ rūpadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ|

618. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ nīlaṃ pītakaṃ lauhitakaṃ audātaṃ kāḷakaṃ mañ‍jiṭṭhakaṃ hari harivaṇṇaṃ ambaṅkuravaṇṇaṃ dīghaṃ rassaṃ aṇuṃ thūlaṃ vaṭṭaṃ parimaṇḍalaṃ caturaṃsaṃ chaḷaṃsaṃ aṭṭhaṃsaṃ sauḷasaṃsaṃ nin‍naṃ thalaṃ chāyā ātapau ālaukau andhakārau abbhā mahikā dhūmau rajau candamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā sūriyamaṇḍalassa  vaṇṇanibhā tārakarūpānaṃ vaṇṇanibhā ādāsamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā maṇisaṅkhamuttāvaiḷuriyassa vaṇṇanibhā jātarūparajatassa vaṇṇanibhā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ, yaṃ rūpaṃ sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ cakkhumhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, rūpaṃ paitaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ paitaṃ rūpadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ|

619. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ? yaṃ  rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ nīlaṃ pītakaṃ lauhitakaṃ audātaṃ kāḷakaṃ mañ‍jiṭṭhakaṃ hari harivaṇṇaṃ ambaṅkuravaṇṇaṃ dīghaṃ rassaṃ aṇuṃ thūlaṃ vaṭṭaṃ parimaṇḍalaṃ caturaṃsaṃ chaḷaṃsaṃ aṭṭhaṃsaṃ sauḷasaṃsaṃ nin‍naṃ thalaṃ chāyā ātapau ālaukau andhakārau abbhā mahikā dhūmau rajau candamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā sūriyamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā tārakarūpānaṃ vaṇṇanibhā ādāsamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā maṇisaṅkhamuttāvaiḷuriyassa vaṇṇanibhā jātarūparajatassa vaṇṇanibhā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ, yaṃ rūpaṃ ārabbha cakkhuṃ nissāya cakkhusamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ rūpaṃ ārabbha cakkhuṃ nissāya cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ rūpārammaṇau cakkhuṃ nissāya cakkhusamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ rūpārammaṇā cakkhuṃ nissāya cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā, rūpaṃ paitaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ paitaṃ rūpadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ|

620. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ saddāyatanaṃ? yau saddau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya  anidassanau sappaṭighau bhairisaddau mudiṅgasaddau saṅkhasaddau paṇavasaddau gītasaddau vāditasaddau sammasaddau pāṇisaddau sattānaṃ nigghausasaddau dhātūnaṃ san‍nighātasaddau vātasaddau udakasaddau manussasaddau amanussasaddau, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi saddau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ saddaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ sautaina anidassanaina sappaṭighaina suṇi vā  suṇāti vā suṇissati vā suṇai vā, saddau paisau saddāyatanaṃ paitaṃ saddadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ saddāyatanaṃ|

621. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ saddāyatanaṃ? yau  saddau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau bhairisaddau mudiṅgasaddau saṅkhasaddau paṇavasaddau gītasaddau vāditasaddau sammasaddau pāṇisaddau sattānaṃ nigghausasaddau dhātūnaṃ san‍nighātasaddau vātasaddau udakasaddau manussasaddau amanussasaddau, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi saddau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ  upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yamhi saddamhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi sautaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, saddau paisau saddāyatanaṃ paitaṃ saddadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ saddāyatanaṃ|

622. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ saddāyatanaṃ? yau saddau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau bhairisaddau mudiṅgasaddau saṅkhasaddau paṇavasaddau gītasaddau vāditasaddau sammasaddau pāṇisaddau sattānaṃ nigghausasaddau dhātūnaṃ san‍nighātasaddau vātasaddau udakasaddau  manussasaddau amanussasaddau, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi saddau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yau saddau anidassanau sappaṭighau sautamhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, saddau paisau saddāyatanaṃ paitaṃ saddadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ saddāyatanaṃ|

623. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ saddāyatanaṃ? yau saddau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau bhairisaddau mudiṅgasaddau saṅkhasaddau paṇavasaddau gītasaddau vāditasaddau sammasaddau pāṇisaddau sattānaṃ nigghausasaddau dhātūnaṃ san‍nighātasaddau vātasaddau udakasaddau manussasaddau amanussasaddau, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi saddau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ saddaṃ ārabbha sautaṃ nissāya sautasamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ saddaṃ ārabbha sautaṃ nissāya sautasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ saddārammaṇau sautaṃ nissāya sautasamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ saddārammaṇā  sautaṃ nissāya sautasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā, saddau paisau saddāyatanaṃ paitaṃ saddadhātu paisā – idaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ saddāyatanaṃ|

624. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ? yau gandhau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau mūlagandhau sāragandhau tacagandhau pattagandhau pupphagandhau phalagandhau āmakagandhau vissagandhau sugandhau duggandhau, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi gandhau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ gandhaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ ghānaina anidassanaina sappaṭighaina ghāyi vā ghāyati vā ghāyissati vā ghāyai vā, gandhau paisau gandhāyatanaṃ paitaṃ gandhadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ|

625. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ? yau gandhau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau mūlagandhau sāragandhau tacagandhau pattagandhau pupphagandhau phalagandhau āmakagandhau vissagandhau sugandhau duggandhau, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi gandhau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yamhi gandhamhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi ghānaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai  vā, gandhau paisau gandhāyatanaṃ paitaṃ gandhadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ|

626. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ? yau gandhau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau mūlagandhau sāragandhau tacagandhau pattagandhau pupphagandhau phalagandhau āmakagandhau vissagandhau sugandhau duggandhau, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi gandhau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yau  gandhau anidassanau sappaṭighau ghānamhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, gandhau paisau gandhāyatanaṃ paitaṃ gandhadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ|

627. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ? yau gandhau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau mūlagandhau sāragandhau tacagandhau pattagandhau pupphagandhau phalagandhau āmakagandhau vissagandhau sugandhau duggandhau, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi gandhau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya  anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ gandhaṃ ārabbha ghānaṃ nissāya ghānasamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ gandhaṃ ārabbha ghānaṃ nissāya ghānasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ gandhārammaṇau ghānaṃ nissāya ghānasamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati  vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ gandhārammaṇā ghānaṃ nissāya ghānasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā, gandhau paisau gandhāyatanaṃ paitaṃ gandhadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ|

628. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rasāyatanaṃ? yau rasau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau mūlarasau khandharasau tacarasau pattarasau puppharasau  phalarasau ambilaṃ madhuraṃ tittakaṃ kaṭukaṃ lauṇikaṃ khārikaṃ lambilaṃ lapilakaṃ (sī॰) kasāvau sādu asādu, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi rasau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ rasaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ jivhāya anidassanāya sappaṭighāya sāyi vā sāyati vā sāyissati vā sāyai vā, rasau paisau rasāyatanaṃ paitaṃ rasadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rasāyatanaṃ|

629. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rasāyatanaṃ? yau rasau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau mūlarasau khandharasau tacarasau pattarasau puppharasau phalarasau ambilaṃ madhuraṃ tittakaṃ kaṭukaṃ lauṇikaṃ khārikaṃ lambilaṃ kasāvau sādu asādu, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi rasau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yamhi rasamhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi jivhā anidassanā sappaṭighā paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, rasau paisau rasāyatanaṃ paitaṃ rasadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rasāyatanaṃ|

630. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ rasāyatanaṃ? yau rasau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau mūlarasau khandharasau tacarasau pattarasau puppharasau phalarasau ambilaṃ madhuraṃ tittakaṃ kaṭukaṃ lauṇikaṃ khārikaṃ lambilaṃ kasāvau sādu asādu, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi rasau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yau rasau anidassanau sappaṭighau jivhāya  anidassanāya sappaṭighāya paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā  paṭihañ‍ñai vā, rasau paisau rasāyatanaṃ paitaṃ rasadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rasāyatanaṃ|

631. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ rasāyatanaṃ? yau rasau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau mūlarasau khandharasau tacarasau pattarasau puppharasau phalarasau ambilaṃ madhuraṃ tittakaṃ kaṭukaṃ lauṇikaṃ khārikaṃ lambilaṃ kasāvau sādu asādu, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi rasau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ rasaṃ ārabbha jivhaṃ nissāya jivhāsamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ rasaṃ ārabbha jivhaṃ nissāya jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ rasārammaṇau jivhaṃ nissāya jivhāsamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ rasārammaṇā jivhaṃ nissāya jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā, rasau paisau rasāyatanaṃ paitaṃ rasadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rasāyatanaṃ|

632. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ itthindriyaṃ? yaṃ itthiyā itthiliṅgaṃ itthinimittaṃ itthikuttaṃ itthākappau itthattaṃ itthibhāvau itthittaṃ itthībhāvau (syā॰) – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ itthindriyaṃ|

633. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ purisindriyaṃ? yaṃ purisassa purisaliṅgaṃ purisanimittaṃ purisakuttaṃ purisākappau purisattaṃ purisabhāvau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ purisindriyaṃ|

634. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ? yau taisaṃ rūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ|

635. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti? yā kusalacittassa vā akusalacittassa vā abyākatacittassa vā abhik‍kamantassa vā paṭik‍kamantassa vā ālaukaintassa vā vilaukaintassa vā samiñ‍jaintassa vā pasāraintassa vā kāyassa thambhanā santhambhanā santhambhitattaṃ viñ‍ñatti viñ‍ñāpanā viñ‍ñāpitattaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti|

636. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ vacīviñ‍ñatti? yā kusalacittassa vā akusalacittassa vā abyākatacittassa vā vācā girā byappathau udīraṇaṃ ghausau ghausakammaṃ vācā vacībhaidau  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati vācā| yā tāya vācāya viñ‍ñatti viñ‍ñāpanā viñ‍ñāpitattaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ vacīviñ‍ñatti|

637. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ākāsadhātu? yau ākāsau ākāsagataṃ aghaṃ aghagataṃ vivarau vivaragataṃ asamphuṭṭhaṃ catūhi mahābhūtaihi – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ākāsadhātu|

638. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa lahutā? yā rūpassa lahutā lahupariṇāmatā adandhanatā avitthanatā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa lahutā|

639. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa mudutā? yā rūpassa mudutā maddavatā akakkhaḷatā akathinatā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa mudutā|

640. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā? yā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā kammañ‍ñattaṃ kammañ‍ñabhāvau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā|

641. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa upacayau? yau āyatanānaṃ ācayau, sau rūpassa upacayau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa upacayau|

642. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa santati? yau rūpassa upacayau, sā rūpassa santati – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa santati|

643. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa jaratā? yā rūpassa jarā jīraṇatā khaṇḍic‍caṃ pālic‍caṃ valittacatā āyunau saṃhāni indriyānaṃ paripākau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa jaratā|

644. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa anic‍catā? yau rūpassa khayau vayau bhaidau paribhaidau anic‍catā antaradhānaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa anic‍catā|

645. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ kabaḷīkārau āhārau? audanau kummāsau sattu macchau maṃsaṃ khīraṃ dadhi sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ yamhi yamhi janapadai taisaṃ taisaṃ  sattānaṃ mukhāsiyaṃ dantavikhādanaṃ galajjhauharaṇīyaṃ kucchivitthambhanaṃ, yāya aujāya sattā yāpainti – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kabaḷīkārau āhārau|

idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā|

upādābhājanīyaṃ|

rūpakaṇḍai paṭhamabhāṇavārau|

646. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ nau upādā? phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, āpaudhātu|

647. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ? pathavīdhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātu kakkhaḷaṃ mudukaṃ saṇhaṃ pharusaṃ sukhasamphassaṃ dukkhasamphassaṃ garukaṃ lahukaṃ, yaṃ phauṭṭhabbaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ kāyaina anidassanaina sappaṭighaina phusi vā phusati vā phusissati vā phusai vā phauṭṭhabbau paisau phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paitaṃ phauṭṭhabbadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ|

648. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ? pathavīdhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātu kakkhaḷaṃ mudukaṃ saṇhaṃ pharusaṃ sukhasamphassaṃ dukkhasamphassaṃ garukaṃ lahukaṃ, yamhi phauṭṭhabbamhi anidassanamhi sappaṭighamhi kāyau anidassanau sappaṭighau paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, phauṭṭhabbau paisau phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paitaṃ phauṭṭhabbadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ|

649. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ? pathavīdhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātu kakkhaḷaṃ mudukaṃ saṇhaṃ pharusaṃ sukhasamphassaṃ dukkhasamphassaṃ garukaṃ lahukaṃ, yau phauṭṭhabbau anidassanau sappaṭighau kāyamhi anidassanamhi  sappaṭighamhi paṭihañ‍ñi vā paṭihañ‍ñati vā paṭihañ‍ñissati vā paṭihañ‍ñai vā, phauṭṭhabbau paisau phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paitaṃ phauṭṭhabbadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ|

650. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ? pathavīdhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātu kakkhaḷaṃ mudukaṃ saṇhaṃ pharusaṃ sukhasamphassaṃ dukkhasamphassaṃ garukaṃ lahukaṃ, yaṃ phauṭṭhabbaṃ ārabbha kāyaṃ nissāya kāyasamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ phauṭṭhabbaṃ ārabbha kāyaṃ nissāya kāyasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ phauṭṭhabbārammaṇau kāyaṃ nissāya kāyasamphassau uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā…pai॰… yaṃ phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā kāyaṃ nissāya kāyasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… caitanā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji vā uppaj‍jati vā uppaj‍jissati vā uppaj‍jai vā, phauṭṭhabbau paisau phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ paitaṃ phauṭṭhabbadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ|

651. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ āpaudhātu? yaṃ  āpau āpaugataṃ sinaihau sinaihagataṃ bandhanattaṃ rūpassa – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ āpaudhātu|

idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ nau upādā|

652. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ sautāyatanaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ jivhāyatanaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ itthindriyaṃ purisindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ|

653. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ? saddāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti rūpassa lahutā rūpassa mudutā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā rūpassa jaratā rūpassa anic‍catā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ na kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ|

654. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ itthindriyaṃ  purisindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ|

655. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ? saddāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti rūpassa lahutā rūpassa mudutā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā rūpassa jaratā rūpassa anic‍catā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ na kammassa katattā, rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ  ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ|

656. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sanidassanaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sanidassanaṃ|

657. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ anidassanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anidassanaṃ|

658. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ sappaṭighaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ sautāyatanaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ jivhāyatanaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ saddāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sappaṭighaṃ|

659. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ appaṭighaṃ? itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ appaṭighaṃ|

660. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ indriyaṃ? cakkhundriyaṃ sautindriyaṃ ghānindriyaṃ jivhindriyaṃ kāyindriyaṃ itthindriyaṃ purisindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ indriyaṃ|

661. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na indriyaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na indriyaṃ|

662. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ mahābhūtaṃ? phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ āpaudhātu – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ mahābhūtaṃ|

663. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ na mahābhūtaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na mahābhūtaṃ|

664. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ viñ‍ñatti? kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ viñ‍ñatti|

665. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na viñ‍ñatti? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na viñ‍ñatti|

666. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ? kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti yaṃ  vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahaitukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ saddāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa lahutā rūpassa mudutā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ|

667. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ itthindriyaṃ purisindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ rūpassa jaratā rūpassa anic‍catā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ na cittajaṃ na cittahaitukaṃ na cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ saddāyatanaṃ  gandhāyatanaṃ  rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa lahutā rūpassa mudutā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ|

668. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cittasahabhu? kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cittasahabhu|

669. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na cittasahabhu? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na cittasahabhu|

670. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cittānuparivatti? kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cittānuparivatti|

671. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ na cittānuparivatti? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na cittānuparivatti|

672. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ|

673. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ|

674. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ auḷārikaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ auḷārikaṃ|

675. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sukhumaṃ? itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sukhumaṃ|

678. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassassa vatthu? cakkhāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassassa vatthu|

679. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassassa na vatthu? sautāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassassa na vatthu|

680. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya  …pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa vatthu? cakkhāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa vatthu|

682. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sautasamphassassa…pai॰… ghānasamphassassa…pai॰… jivhāsamphassassa…pai॰… kāyasamphassassa vatthu? kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyasamphassassa vatthu|

684. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ kāyasamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa vatthu? kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa vatthu|

686. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassassa ārammaṇaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassassa ārammaṇaṃ|

687. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassassa na ārammaṇaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassassa na ārammaṇaṃ|

688. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhusamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ|

692. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyasamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰…  kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ? phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ|

693. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa na ārammaṇaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa na ārammaṇaṃ|

694. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cakkhu catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau…pai॰… suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ|

695. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na cakkhāyatanaṃ? sautāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na cakkhāyatanaṃ|

696. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sautāyatanaṃ…pai॰… ghānāyatanaṃ…pai॰… jivhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ? yau kāyau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau…pai॰… suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ|

697. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na kāyāyatanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na kāyāyatanaṃ|

698. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā…pai॰… rūpadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ|

699. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na rūpāyatanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na rūpāyatanaṃ|

700. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ saddāyatanaṃ…pai॰… gandhāyatanaṃ  …pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ? pathavīdhātu…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ|

701. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ|

702. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhudhātu? cakkhāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhudhātu|

704. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ sautadhātu…pai॰… ghānadhātu…pai॰… jivhādhātu…pai॰… kāyadhātu? kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyadhātu|

705. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na kāyadhātu? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na kāyadhātu|

708. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ saddadhātu…pai॰… gandhadhātu…pai॰… rasadhātu…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbadhātu? phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ phauṭṭhabbadhātu|

709. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na phauṭṭhabbadhātu? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na phauṭṭhabbadhātu|

710. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ? yaṃ cakkhu catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau…pai॰… suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ|

711. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na cakkhundriyaṃ? sautāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na cakkhundriyaṃ|

712. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ sautindriyaṃ…pai॰… ghānindriyaṃ…pai॰… jivhindriyaṃ…pai॰… kāyindriyaṃ? yau kāyau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau…pai॰… suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyindriyaṃ|

713. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na kāyindriyaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na kāyindriyaṃ|

714. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ itthindriyaṃ? yaṃ  itthiyā itthiliṅgaṃ itthinimittaṃ itthikuttaṃ itthākappau itthattaṃ itthibhāvau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ itthindriyaṃ|

715. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ na itthindriyaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na itthindriyaṃ|

716. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ purisindriyaṃ? yaṃ purisassa purisaliṅgaṃ purisanimittaṃ purisakuttaṃ purisākappau purisattaṃ purisabhāvau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ purisindriyaṃ|

717. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na purisindriyaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na purisindriyaṃ|

718. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ? yau taisaṃ rūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ|

719. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na jīvitindriyaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na jīvitindriyaṃ|

720. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti? yā kusalacittassa vā akusalacittassa vā abyākatacittassa vā abhik‍kamantassa vā paṭik‍kamantassa vā ālaukaintassa vā vilaukaintassa vā samiñ‍jaintassa  vā pasāraintassa vā kāyassa thambhanā santhambhanā santhambhitattaṃ viñ‍ñatti viñ‍ñāpanā viñ‍ñāpitattaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti|

721. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na kāyaviñ‍ñatti? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na kāyaviñ‍ñatti|

722. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ vacīviñ‍ñatti? yā kusalacittassa vā akusalacittassa vā abyākatacittassa vā vācā girā byappathau udīraṇaṃ ghausau ghausakammaṃ vācā vacībhaidau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati vācā| yā tāya vācāya viñ‍ñatti viñ‍ñāpanā viñ‍ñāpitattaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ vacīviñ‍ñatti|

723. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na vacīviñ‍ñatti? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na vacīviñ‍ñatti|

724. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ākāsadhātu? yau ākāsau ākāsagataṃ aghaṃ aghagataṃ vivarau vivaragataṃ asamphuṭṭhaṃ catūhi mahābhūtaihi – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ākāsadhātu|

725. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ na ākāsadhātu? cakkhāyatanaṃ  …pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na ākāsadhātu|

726. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ āpaudhātu? yaṃ āpau āpaugataṃ sinaihau sinaihagataṃ bandhanattaṃ rūpassa – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ āpaudhātu|

727. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na āpaudhātu? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na āpaudhātu|

728. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa lahutā? yā rūpassa lahutā lahupariṇāmatā adandhanatā avitthanatā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa lahutā|

729. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na lahutā? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na lahutā|

730. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa mudutā? yā rūpassa mudutā maddavatā akakkhaḷatā akathinatā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa mudutā|

731. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na mudutā? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na mudutā|

732. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā? yā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā kammañ‍ñattaṃ kammañ‍ñabhāvau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā|

733. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na kammañ‍ñatā? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na kammañ‍ñatā|

734. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa upacayau? yau āyatanānaṃ ācayau, sau rūpassa upacayau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa upacayau|

735. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na upacayau? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na upacayau|

736. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa santati? yau rūpassa upacayau, sā rūpassa santati – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa santati|

737. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na santati? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na santati|

738. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa jaratā? yā rūpassa jarā jīraṇatā khaṇḍic‍caṃ pālic‍caṃ valittacatā āyunau saṃhāni indriyānaṃ paripākau  – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa jaratā|

739. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na jaratā? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na jaratā|

740. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa anic‍catā? yau rūpassa khayau vayau bhaidau paribhaidau anic‍catā antaradhānaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa anic‍catā|

741. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na anic‍catā? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ rūpassa na anic‍catā|

742. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kabaḷīkārau āhārau? audanau kummāsau sattu macchau maṃsaṃ khīraṃ dadhi sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ yamhi yamhi janapadai taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ mukhāsiyaṃ dantavikhādanaṃ galajjhauharaṇīyaṃ kucchivitthambhanaṃ, yāya aujāya sattā yāpainti – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ kabaḷīkārau āhārau|

743. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ na kabaḷīkārau āhārau? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rūpassa anic‍catā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ na kabaḷīkārau āhārau|

744. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ upādā? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ upādā|

745. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ upādā? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ upādā|

746. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ nau upādā? phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ  āpaudhātu – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ nau upādā|

747. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ|

748. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ upādiṇṇaṃ? itthindriyaṃ purisindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ upādiṇṇaṃ|

749. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ? saddāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti rūpassa  lahutā rūpassa mudutā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā rūpassa jaratā rūpassa anic‍catā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ na kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ|

750. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ| cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ|

751. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ? itthindriyaṃ purisindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ|

752. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ? saddāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti rūpassa lahutā rūpassa mudutā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā rūpassa jaratā rūpassa anic‍catā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ na kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ|

753. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ anidassanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ anidassanaṃ|

754. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ sanidassanaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ sanidassanaṃ|

755. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ anidassanaṃ? saddāyatanaṃ  …pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ anidassanaṃ|

756. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ sappaṭighaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ sappaṭighaṃ|

757. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ sappaṭighaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ sappaṭighaṃ|

758. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ appaṭighaṃ? itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ appaṭighaṃ|

759. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ indriyaṃ? cakkhundriyaṃ…pai॰… kāyindriyaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ indriyaṃ|

760. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ indriyaṃ? itthindriyaṃ purisindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ indriyaṃ|

761. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na indriyaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau  āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na indriyaṃ|

762. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na mahābhūtaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na mahābhūtaṃ|

763. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ mahābhūtaṃ? phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ āpaudhātu – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ mahābhūtaṃ|

764. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na mahābhūtaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na mahābhūtaṃ|

765. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na viñ‍ñatti? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na viñ‍ñatti?

766. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ viñ‍ñatti? kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ viñ‍ñatti|

767. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na viñ‍ñatti? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na viñ‍ñatti|

768. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ|

769. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ? kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahaitukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ saddāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa lahutā rūpassa mudutā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ|

770. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ? itthindriyaṃ purisindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ rūpassa jaratā rūpassa anic‍catā, yaṃ  vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ na cittajaṃ na cittahaitukaṃ na cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ saddāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa lahutā rūpassa mudutā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ|

771. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na cittasahabhu? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na cittasahabhu|

772. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cittasahabhu? kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cittasahabhu|

773. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na cittasahabhu? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na cittasahabhu?

774. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na cittānuparivatti? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na cittānuparivatti|

775. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cittānuparivatti? kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cittānuparivatti|

776. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na cittānuparivatti? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na cittānuparivatti|

777. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ auḷārikaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ  …pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ auḷārikaṃ|

778. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ auḷārikaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ auḷārikaṃ|

779. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ sukhumaṃ? itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau  āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ sukhumaṃ|

780. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ santikai? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ santikai|

781. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ dūrai? itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ dūrai|

782. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ santikai? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ santikai|

783. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cakkhusamphassassa na vatthu? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cakkhusamphassassa na vatthu|

784. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhusamphassassa vatthu? cakkhāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhusamphassassa vatthu|

785. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhusamphassassa na vatthu? sautāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhusamphassassa na vatthu|

786. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cakkhusamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu|

787. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa vatthu? cakkhāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa vatthu|

788. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu? sautāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu?

789. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ sautasamphassassa…pai॰… ghānasamphassassa…pai॰… jivhāsamphassassa…pai॰… kāyasamphassassa na vatthu? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kāyasamphassassa na vatthu|

790. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyasamphassassa vatthu? kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyasamphassassa vatthu|

791. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyasamphassassa na vatthu? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… jivhāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyasamphassassa na vatthu|

792. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kāyasamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu|

793. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa vatthu? kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa vatthu|

794. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… jivhāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa na vatthu|

795. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhusamphassassa na ārammaṇaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhusamphassassa na ārammaṇaṃ|

796. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cakkhusamphassassa ārammaṇaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cakkhusamphassassa ārammaṇaṃ|

797. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cakkhusamphassassa na ārammaṇaṃ? saddāyatanaṃ…pai॰…  kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cakkhusamphassassa na ārammaṇaṃ|

798. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhusamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰… cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa na ārammaṇaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa na ārammaṇaṃ|

799. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ|

800. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa na ārammaṇaṃ? saddāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa na ārammaṇaṃ|

801. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ sautasamphassassa…pai॰… ghānasamphassassa…pai॰… jivhāsamphassassa…pai॰… kāyasamphassassa na ārammaṇaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰…  kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyasamphassassa na ārammaṇaṃ|

802. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kāyasamphassassa ārammaṇaṃ? phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kāyasamphassassa ārammaṇaṃ|

803. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kāyasamphassassa na ārammaṇaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kāyasamphassassa na ārammaṇaṃ|

804. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyasamphassajāya vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… caitanāya…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa na ārammaṇaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰…  kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa na ārammaṇaṃ|

805. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ? phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ  – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇaṃ|

806. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa na ārammaṇaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kāyaviñ‍ñāṇassa na ārammaṇaṃ|

807. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na cakkhāyatanaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na cakkhāyatanaṃ|

808. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cakkhu catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau…pai॰… suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ|

809. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na cakkhāyatanaṃ? sautāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na cakkhāyatanaṃ|

810. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na sautāyatanaṃ…pai॰… na ghānāyatanaṃ…pai॰… na jivhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… na kāyāyatanaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na kāyāyatanaṃ|

811. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ? yau kāyau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau…pai॰… suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ|

812. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na kāyāyatanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… jivhāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na kāyāyatanaṃ|

813. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na rūpāyatanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ  – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na rūpāyatanaṃ|

814. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpāyatanaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā…pai॰… rūpadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpāyatanaṃ|

815. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na rūpāyatanaṃ? saddāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na rūpāyatanaṃ|

816. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na saddāyatanaṃ…pai॰…  na gandhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… na rasāyatanaṃ…pai॰… na phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ|

817. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ? pathavīdhātu…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbadhātu paisā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ|

818. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ|

819. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na cakkhudhātu? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na cakkhudhātu|

820. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhudhātu? cakkhāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhudhātu|

821. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na cakkhudhātu? sautāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na cakkhudhātu|

822. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na sautadhātu…pai॰… na ghānadhātu…pai॰… na jivhādhātu…pai॰… na kāyadhātu? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na kāyadhātu|

823. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ  ajjhattikaṃ kāyadhātu? kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyadhātu|

824. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na kāyadhātu? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… jivhāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na kāyadhātu|

825. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na rūpadhātu? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na rūpadhātu|

826. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpadhātu? rūpāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpadhātu|

827. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na rūpadhātu? saddāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na rūpadhātu|

828. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na saddadhātu…pai॰…  na gandhadhātu…pai॰… na rasadhātu  …pai॰… na phauṭṭhabbadhātu? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na phauṭṭhabbadhātu|

829. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ phauṭṭhabbadhātu? phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ phauṭṭhabbadhātu|

830. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na phauṭṭhabbadhātu? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na phauṭṭhabbadhātu|

831. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na cakkhundriyaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na cakkhundriyaṃ|

832. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ? yaṃ cakkhu catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau…pai॰… suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ|

833. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na cakkhundriyaṃ? sautāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na cakkhundriyaṃ|

834. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na sautindriyaṃ…pai॰… na ghānindriyaṃ…pai॰… na jivhindriyaṃ…pai॰… na kāyindriyaṃ? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na kāyindriyaṃ|

835. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyindriyaṃ? yau kāyau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau…pai॰… suñ‍ñau gāmau paisau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ kāyindriyaṃ|

836. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na kāyindriyaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… jivhāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na kāyindriyaṃ|

837. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na itthindriyaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na itthindriyaṃ|

838. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ itthindriyaṃ? yaṃ itthiyā itthiliṅgaṃ itthinimittaṃ itthikuttaṃ itthākappau itthattaṃ itthibhāvau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ itthindriyaṃ|

841. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ purisindriyaṃ? yaṃ purisassa purisaliṅgaṃ purisanimittaṃ purisakuttaṃ purisākappau purisattaṃ purisabhāvau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ purisindriyaṃ|

844. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ jīvitindriyaṃ? yau taisaṃ rūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ jīvitindriyaṃ|

846. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na kāyaviñ‍ñatti? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na kāyaviñ‍ñatti|

847. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti? yā kusalacittassa vā akusalacittassa vā abyākatacittassa vā abhik‍kamantassa vā paṭik‍kamantassa vā ālaukaintassa vā vilaukaintassa vā samiñ‍jaintassa vā pasāraintassa vā kāyassa thambhanā santhambhanā santhambhitattaṃ viñ‍ñatti viñ‍ñāpanā viñ‍ñāpitattaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti|

848. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na kāyaviñ‍ñatti? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na kāyaviñ‍ñatti|

849. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na vacīviñ‍ñatti? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na vacīviñ‍ñatti|

850. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ vacīviñ‍ñatti? yā kusalacittassa vā akusalacittassa vā abyākatacittassa vā vācā girā byappathau udīraṇaṃ dhausau ghausakammaṃ vācā vacībhaidau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati vācā| yā tāya vācāya viñ‍ñatti viñ‍ñāpanā viñ‍ñāpitattaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ vacīviñ‍ñatti|

853. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ ākāsadhātu? yau ākāsau ākāsagataṃ aghaṃ aghagataṃ vivarau vivaragataṃ asamphuṭṭhaṃ catūhi mahābhūtaihi – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ ākāsadhātu|

856. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ āpaudhātu? yaṃ āpau āpaugataṃ sinaihau sinaihagataṃ bandhanattaṃ rūpassa – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ āpaudhātu|

857. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na āpaudhātu? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na āpaudhātu|

858. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ rūpassa na lahutā? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ rūpassa na lahutā|

859. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa lahutā? yā rūpassa lahutā lahupariṇāmatā adandhanatā avitthanatā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa lahutā|

860. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa na lahutā? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa na lahutā|

864. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ rūpassa na kammañ‍ñatā? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ rūpassa na kammañ‍ñatā|

868. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa upacayau? yau āyatanānaṃ ācayau, sau rūpassa upacayau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa upacayau|

871. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa santati? yau rūpassa upacayau, sā rūpassa santati – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa santati|

872. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa na santati? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa na santati|

873. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ rūpassa na jaratā? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ rūpassa na jaratā|

874. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa jaratā? yā rūpassa jarā jīraṇatā khaṇḍic‍caṃ pālic‍caṃ valittacatā āyunau saṃhāni indriyānaṃ paripākau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa jaratā|

875. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa na jaratā? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa na jaratā|

876. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ rūpassa na anic‍catā? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ rūpassa na anic‍catā|

877. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa anic‍catā? yau rūpassa khayau vayau bhaidau paribhaidau anic‍catā antaradhānaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa anic‍catā|

878. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa na anic‍catā? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ rūpassa na anic‍catā|

879. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na kabaḷīkārau āhārau? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ ajjhattikaṃ na kabaḷīkārau āhārau|

880. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kabaḷīkārau āhārau? audanau kummāsau sattu macchau maṃsaṃ khīraṃ dadhi sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ yamhi yamhi  janapadai taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ mukhāsiyaṃ dantavikhādanaṃ galajjhauharaṇīyaṃ kucchivitthambhanaṃ yāya aujāya sattā yāpainti – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ kabaḷīkārau āhārau|

881. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na kabaḷīkārau āhārau? rūpāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rūpassa anic‍catā – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ bāhiraṃ na kabaḷīkārau āhārau|

catuk‍kaṃ

882. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā upādiṇṇaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ, itthindriyaṃ purisindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā upādiṇṇaṃ|

883. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā anupādiṇṇaṃ? saddāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti rūpassa lahutā rūpassa mudutā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā rūpassa jaratā rūpassa anic‍catā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ na kammassa katattā  rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā anupādiṇṇaṃ|

884. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ nau upādā upādiṇṇaṃ? kammassa katattā phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ āpaudhātu – idaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ nau upādā upādiṇṇaṃ|

885. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ nau upādā anupādiṇṇaṃ? na kammassa katattā phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ āpaudhātu – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ nau upādā anupādiṇṇaṃ|

886. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ, itthindriyaṃ purisindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ|

887. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ? saddāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti rūpassa lahutā rūpassa mudutā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā rūpassa  jaratā rūpassa anic‍catā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ na kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ|

888. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ nau upādā upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ? kammassa katattā phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ āpaudhātu – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ nau upādā upādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ|

889. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ nau upādā anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ? na kammassa katattā phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ āpaudhātu – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ nau upādā anupādiṇṇupādāniyaṃ|

890. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā sappaṭighaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā sappaṭighaṃ|

891. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā  appaṭighaṃ? itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā appaṭighaṃ|

892. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ nau upādā sappaṭighaṃ? phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ nau upādā sappaṭighaṃ|

899. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ upādā santikai? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… rasāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādā santikai|

902. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ sanidassanaṃ? kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ sanidassanaṃ|

903. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ anidassanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ, itthindriyaṃ purisindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ kammassa katattā  gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ anidassanaṃ|

904. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ sanidassanaṃ? na kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ sanidassanaṃ|

905. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ anidassanaṃ? saddāyatanaṃ kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti rūpassa lahutā rūpassa mudutā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā rūpassa jaratā rūpassa anic‍catā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ na kammassa katattā gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ anidassanaṃ|

906. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ sappaṭighaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… kāyāyatanaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ sappaṭighaṃ|

907. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ appaṭighaṃ? itthindriyaṃ purisindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ kammassa katattā ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ appaṭighaṃ|

908. katamaṃ  taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ sappaṭighaṃ? saddāyatanaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ na kammassa katattā rūpāyatanaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ sappaṭighaṃ|

909. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ appaṭighaṃ? kāyaviñ‍ñatti vacīviñ‍ñatti  rūpassa lahutā rūpassa mudutā rūpassa kammañ‍ñatā rūpassa jaratā rūpassa anic‍catā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi  atthi rūpaṃ na kammassa katattā ākāsadhātu āpaudhātu rūpassa upacayau rūpassa santati kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ anupādiṇṇaṃ appaṭighaṃ|

910. katamaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ mahābhūtaṃ? kammassa katattā phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ āpaudhātu – idaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ upādiṇṇaṃ mahābhūtaṃ|
